n0. 1673 ka 
title śrīvidyānityapaddhati 
author sāhibakaula 
extent 433** patra 
subject tantra 

pṛ0 1a) oṃśrīdevyaisiddhidātryainamaḥ śrībhairavuvāca a
dhunāpaddhatiṃvakṣyeśrīvidyārcanasaṃyutāṃ gadyapadyama
yīṃgopyāṃsakalāgamaniścitāṃ prātaḥkṛtyamakṛ
tvātuyodevīṃbhaktitorcayet tasyapūjātuvipha
lāśaucahīnāyathākriyā tataḥśrīmānsādha
kobrāhmemuhūrteśayanatalādutthāya karacara
ṇādiprakṣālyācamya nijāsanesamupaviśyani

pṛ0 1ba) jaśirasi vrahmaraṃdhrastha śvetavarṇā'dhomukha sahasradalaka
malakarṇikāṃtargata caṃdramaṃḍalasiṃhāsanopari niṣaṇṇaṃ 
svaguruṃśuklavarṇaṃśuklālaṃkārabhūṣitaṃ jñānānaṃdamuditamā
nasaṃsaccidānaṃdavigrahaṃ trinetraṃcaturbhujaṃjñānamudrāpusta-
kavarābhayakaraṃ vāmāṃgavāmahastadhṛtaraktakamalayāraktava
sanābharaṇayā svapriyayādakṣiṇabhujenāliṃgitaṃ śvetavastrā
bharaṇānulepitaṃmuktādāmādibhūṣitaṃcaṃdrakāṃtanibhaṃ sarvade

pṛ0 2a) vadevaṃsarvatīrthatīrthaṃ paramaśivasvarūpaṃśāṃtaṃsvaprasannaṃdhyā
tvā taccaraṇakamalayugalavigaladamṛtadhārabhiḥ svātmānaṃ
pariplutaṃvibhāvya manasāgaṃdhapuṣpadhūpadīpanaivedyādibhi
rabhyarcya praṇamyaca oṃasyaśrīgurumaṃtrasya sanatkumārṛ
ṣiḥ(śi) paṃkticchaṃdaḥ(mu) śrīamukānaṃdanāthodevatā(hṛ) aiṃvījaṃ(gu) hrīṃ
śaktiḥ(pā) śrīṃkīlakaṃ(vā) mamaśrīdharmārthakāmamokṣārthejapevi
niyogaḥ(sa) itikṛtāṃjaliḥsmṛtvānyaset athakaraṣaḍaṃganyā

pṛ0 2ba) saḥ oṃaiṃhrīṃśrīṃaṃguṣṭhābhyāṃnamaḥ hṛdayāyanamaḥ hskhphreṃ
tarjanībhyāṃsvāhā śirasesvāhā hsrkṣmlvyūṃmadhyamā
bhyāṃvaṣaṭ śikhāyaivaṣaṭ shkhphreṃanāmikābhyāṃhuṃ kava
cāyahuṃ shkṣmlvryūṃkaniṣṭhikābhyāṃvauṣaṭ netratrayā
yavauṣaṭ hsauṃḥshauṃḥamukānaṃdanāthaśrīamukāṃvāśrīpādu-
kāṃpūjayāminamaḥkaratalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃphaṭ astrāyaphaṭ 
itikaraṣaḍaṃgaḥ oṃaiṃhrīṃśrīṃhasakhaphreṃhasarakṣamalavayūṃ

pṛ0 3a) sahakhaphreṃsaharakṣamalavayūṃhsauṃḥshauṃḥ śrīmacchrīsvāmipra
tāpakaulānaṃdanāthaśrīpādukāṃśrīdayāvatyaṃvāśrīpādukāṃ
pūjayāminamaḥ itidaśadhāvimṛṣya daṃḍavatpraṇāmaṃmanasā
kuryāt sahasradalapaṃkajesakalaśītaraśmiprabhaṃvarābhaya
karāṃvujaṃvimalagaṃdhapuṣpāṃvaraṃ prasannavadanekṣaṇaṃsakaladeva
tārūpiṇaṃsmareśihasihaṃsagaṃtadabhidhānapūrvaṃguruṃ 1 śvetaṃśve
tavilepamālyavasanaṃvāmenaraktotpalaṃvibhratyāpriyayeta

pṛ0 3ba) reṇattarasāśliṣṭaṃprasannānanaṃ hastābhyāmabhayaṃvaraṃcadadha
taṃśaṃbhusvarūpaṃparaṃhālālohitalocanotpalayugaṃvaṃde
śiraḥsthaṃguruṃ itidhyātvā mānasopacāraiḥguruṃsaṃpūjya laṃ
pṛthivyātmakaṃgaṃdhaṃśrīgurumūrtayesa0 haṃākāśātmakaṃpuṣpaṃ
śrīgurumūrtayesa0 yaṃvāyvātmakaṃdhūpaṃśrīgurumūrtayesa0 raṃva
hnyātmakaṃdīpaṃśrīgurumūrtayesa0 vaṃamṛtātmakaṃnānānaivedyaṃ
śrīgurumūrtayesa0 saṃsarvātmakaṃphalamūlatāṃvūlaṃśrīgurumū

pṛ0 4a) rtayesamarpayāminamaḥ itimānasopacāraiḥsaṃpūjya oṃnamā
misadguruṃśāṃtaṃpratyakṣaṃśivarūpiṇaṃ śirasāyogapīṭhasthaṃ
muktikāmārthasiddhaye 1 śrīguruṃparamānaṃdaṃvaṃdāmyānaṃdavi
grahaṃ yasyasānnidhyamātreṇacidānaṃdāyateparaṃ 2 namostugu
ravetasmāyiṣṭadevasvarūpiṇe yasyavāgamṛtaṃhaṃtiviṣaṃsaṃ
sārasaṃjñakaṃ 3 akhaṃḍamaṃḍalākāraṃvyāptaṃyenacarācaraṃ tatpadaṃ
darśitaṃyenatasmaiśrīguravenamaḥ 4 ajñānatimirāṃdhasyajñā-

pṛ0 4ba) nāṃjanaśalākayā cakṣurunmīlitaṃyenatasmaiśrīguravenamaḥ 5 
gururvrahmāgururviṣṇurguruḥsākṣātmaheśvaraḥ gururevajagatsarvaṃ
tasmaiśrīguravenamaḥ 6 navāyanavarūpāyaparamārthaikarūpiṇe 
sarvājñānatamobhedabhānadecidghanāyate 7 namastenāthabhaga
vanśivāyagururūpiṇe vidyāvatārasaṃsiddhyaisvīkṛtānekavi
grahaḥ 8 svataṃtrāyadayākḷptavigrahāyaparātmane parataṃtrāyabha
ktānāṃbhavyānāṃbhavyarūpiṇe 9 vivekināṃvivekāyavimarśāya

pṛ0 5a) vimarśināṃ prakāśināṃprakāśāyajñānināṃjñānarūpiṇe 10 pu
rastātpārśvayoḥpṛṣṭhenamaskuryāmuparyadhaḥ sadāsaccittarūpeṇavi
dhehibhavadāsanaṃ 11 tvatprasādādahaṃdevakṛtakṛtyosmisarvataḥ 
māyāmṛtyumahāpāśāvimuktosmiśivosmica 12 ityetairmaṃtraiḥ
śrīguruṃdaṃḍavatmanasāpraṇamya suprasannaṃvibhāvya manasātadā-
jñāṃgṛhītvā mūlādhāresvarṇavarṇacaturdalakamalakarṇikāṃta
rgataśrṛṃgāṭapīṭhāṃtargata trikoṇapīṭhopari parāṃśaktiṃkuṃḍalinīṃ

pṛ0 5ba) sarpākārāmūrdhvamukhīṃsārdhatrivalayāṃ udyaddinakarasahasrabhā
svarāṃvidyutkoṭisannibhāṃvisataṃtutanīyasīṃ paṃcāśadvarṇavigra
hāmaṣṭātriṃśatkalārūpiṇīṃ tridhāmadhāmānaṃsarvaprāṇijīvāṃ 
sarvadevādhidevīṃsakalamaṃtramātaraṃsuptāṃvibhāvya gurūpadiṣṭhamā
rgeṇanijasahajanādenasacaitanyāṃvidhāya tatra caturdaleṣu oṃaiṃhrīṃśrīṃ
vaṃnamaḥ 4ṃśaṃnamaḥ 4ṃṣaṃnamaḥ 4ṃsaṃnamaḥ itipatreṣu madhyemūlenaca
prāgdakṣiṇyenasaṃpūjya haṃsaḥitimaṃtreṇakamalātkamalāṃtaraṃnītvā 

pṛ0 6a) tataḥsvādhiṣṭhāneliṃgamūlevidrumavarṇeṣaṭdalakamaletāṃnī
tvā tatra 4ṃvaṃnamaḥ 4ṃbhaṃnamaḥ 4ṃmaṃnamaḥ 4ṃyaṃnamaḥ 4ṃraṃnamaḥ 4ṃlaṃna
maḥ itipatreṣumadhyemūlenacasaṃpūjya tatomaṇipūrakenābhau 
nīlavarṇedaśadalakamaletāṃnītvā tatra 4ṃḍaṃnamaḥ 4ṃḍhaṃnamaḥ 
4ṃṇaṃnamaḥ 4ṃtaṃnamaḥ 4ṃthaṃnamaḥ 4ṃdaṃnamaḥ 4ṃdhaṃnamaḥ 4ṃnaṃnamaḥ 4ṃpaṃ
namaḥ 4ṃphaṃnamaḥ itipatreṣumadhyemūlenacasaṃpūjya tatovakṣasya
'nāhatepiṃgalavarṇedvādaśadalakamaletāṃnītvā tatra 4ṃkaṃnamaḥ 

pṛ0 6ba) 4ṃkhaṃnamaḥ 4ṃgaṃnamaḥ 4ṃghaṃnamaḥ 4ṃṅaṃnamaḥ 4ṃcaṃnamaḥ 4ṃchaṃnamaḥ 4ṃ
jaṃnamaḥ 4ṃjhaṃnamaḥ 4ṃñaṃnamaḥ 4ṃṭaṃnamaḥ 4ṃṭhaṃnamaḥ itipatreṣumadhye
mūlenacasaṃpūjya tatoviśuddhaukaṃṭhedhūmravarṇeṣoḍaśadalakama
letāṃnītvā tatra 4ṃaṃnamaḥ 4ṃāṃnamaḥ 4ṃiṃnamaḥ 4ṃīṃnamaḥ 4ṃuṃnamaḥ 
4ṃūṃnamaḥ 4ṃṛṃnamaḥ 4ṃṛnamaḥ 4ṃḷṃnamaḥ 4ṃḹṃnamaḥ 4ṃeṃnamaḥ 4ṃaiṃnamaḥ 
4ṃoṃnamaḥ 4ṃauṃnamaḥ 4ṃaṃnamaḥ 4ṃaṃḥnamaḥ itipatreṣumadhyemūlena
casaṃpūjya tatobhrūmadhye ājñācakrevidyudvarṇedvidalakamaletāṃ

pṛ0 7a) nītvā tatra 4ṃhaṃnamaḥ 4ṃkṣaṃnamaḥ itipatreṣumadhyemūlenacasaṃpū
jya tatovrahmaraṃdhragatasahasradalakamalaṃprāpayya tatra viṃdu
rūpatejomayaparamaśivenasahaikatāṃnītvā tataḥsravatāparamā
mṛtenatāṃsaṃtarpya tatranādaśravaṇatatparomuhūrtaṃlayaṃvibhāvya 
tatovarohesohamitimaṃtreṇatāṃkuṃḍalinīṃ ājñācakrevidyu
dvarṇedvidalakamalesamānīya tatra 4ṃhaṃnamaḥ 4ṃkṣaṃnamaḥ itipatra
yoḥmadhyemūlenacasaṃpūjya tatoviśuddhaukaṃṭhedhūmravarṇeṣoḍaśada

pṛ0 7ba) lakamaletāsamānīya tatra 4ṃaṃnamaḥ 4ṃāṃnamaḥ 4ṃiṃnamaḥ 4ṃīṃnamaḥ 
4ṃuṃnamaḥ 4ṃūṃnamaḥ 4ṃṛṃnamaḥ 4ṃṛnamaḥ 4ṃḷṃnamaḥ 4ṃḹṃnamaḥ 4ṃeṃ
namaḥ 4ṃaiṃnamaḥ 4ṃoṃnamaḥ 4ṃauṃnamaḥ 4ṃaṃnamaḥ 4ṃaṃḥnamaḥ itipa
treṣumadhyemūlenacābhyarcya tatovakṣasya'nāhatepiṃgalavarṇedvāda
śadalakamalesamānīya tatra 4ṃkaṃnamaḥ 4ṃkhaṃnamaḥ 4ṃgaṃnamaḥ 4ṃghaṃ
namaḥ 4ṃṅaṃnamaḥ 4ṃcaṃnamaḥ 4ṃchaṃnamaḥ 4ṃjaṃnamaḥ 4ṃjhaṃnamaḥ 4ṃñaṃnamaḥ 4ṃṭaṃ
namaḥ 4ṃṭhaṃnamaḥ itipatreṣumadhyemūlenacābhyarcya tatomaṇipūrenā

pṛ0 8a) bhaunīlavarṇedaśadalakamaletāṃsamānīya tatra 4ṃḍaṃnamaḥ 4ṃḍhaṃna
maḥ 4ṃṇaṃnamaḥ 4ṃtaṃnamaḥ 4ṃthaṃnamaḥ 4ṃdaṃnamaḥ 4ṃdhaṃnamaḥ 4ṃnaṃnamaḥ 4ṃpaṃna
maḥ 4ṃphaṃnamaḥ itipatreṣumadhyemūlenacasaṃpūjya tataḥsvādhiṣṭhāneliṃ
gamūlevidrumavarṇeṣaṭdalakamaletāṃsamānīya tatra 4ṃvaṃnamaḥ 4ṃ
bhaṃnamaḥ 4ṃmaṃnamaḥ 4ṃyaṃnamaḥ 4ṃraṃnamaḥ 4ṃlaṃnamaḥ itipatreṣumadhyemūle
nacābhyarcya tatomūlādhāresvarṇavarṇecaturdalakamaletāṃsamānīya 
tatrasthāpayet tatomūlādhāre tatra 4ṃvaṃnamaḥ 4ṃśaṃnamaḥ 4ṃṣaṃnamaḥ 

pṛ0 8ba) 4ṃsaṃnamaḥ madhyemūlenatāṃsamarcayet ityārohāvarohakrameṇa
parāṃśaktiṃkuṃḍalinīṃdhyātvā prakāśamānāṃprathameprayāṇeprati
prayāṇepyamṛtāvasānāṃ aṃtaḥpadavyāmanusaṃcaraṃtīmānaṃdarū
pāmavalāprapadye 1 nāhamasminacānyosmivrahmaivāhaṃnaśoka-
bhāk saccidānaṃdarūpohaṃsvātmānamiticiṃtayet hṛtmaṃtreṇa
layaṃvibhāvayet mūlādhārevahnimaṃḍaledaśakalātmakaṃvibhā
vya tatramūlavidyāyāḥprathamakūṭaṃvāgbhavākhyaṃsaṃsphurattaḍillatā

pṛ0 9a) bhāsvaraṃanāhataparyaṃtaprāptanādaśikhaṃvibhāvya anāhatesū
ryamaṃḍalaṃdvādaśakalātmakaṃdhyātvā tanmadhyemūlavidyāyāḥkāma
rājyākhyaṃdvitīyakūṭaṃ udyadādityasaṃkāśamājñāparyaṃtaprāptanā
daśikhaṃadhūrdhvaṃprasṛtaraśmikaṃdhyātvā ājñāyāṃsomamaṃḍalaṃṣo
ḍaśakalātmakaṃvibhāvya tanmadhyemūlavidyāyāḥśaktikūṭākhyaṃ
tṛtīyaṃkhaṃḍaṃśaśiprabhaṃvrahmaraṃdhrāṃtargatavidūrdhvamaṃḍalaparyaṃtaprāpta
nādaśikhaṃdhyātvā tatomūlādhārādivrahmaraṃdhrāṃtaṃ mūlavidyāṃ 

pṛ0 9ba) udyaddinakarasahasrabhāsvarāṃtejomayīṃmakhaṃḍāṃdhyātvā tatte
jasāvyāptaṃsvadehaṃviciṃtya tattejasāpariṇatāṃśrīparadevatāṃhṛda
yakamaledhyātvā mānasopacāraiḥsaṃpūjya laṃpṛthivyātmakaṃgaṃdhaṃ
śrīmahātripurasuṃdarīśrīpādu्kāyaisamarpayāminamaḥ haṃākā
śātmakaṃpuṣpaṃśrīmahātripurasuṃdarīśrī0 yaṃvāyvātmakaṃdhūpaṃśrī
mahātripurasuṃdarīśrī0 raṃvahnyātmakaṃdīpaṃśrīmahātripurasuṃdarī
śrī0 vaṃamṛtātmakaṃnānānaivedyaṃśrīmahātripurasuṃdarīśrī0 saṃsa

pṛ0 10a) rvātmakaṃphalamūlatāṃvūlādikaṃśrīmahātripurasuṃdarīśrīpādukā
yaisamarpayāminamaḥ prāṇāyāmatrayaṃkuryādyathā tatravihitapa
dmādyāsanaḥsādhakomūlādhārārpitavittavṛtti mūlaṃpraṇavaṃvā
ṣoḍaśavāramuccaran dakṣāṃguṣṭenadakṣanāsāpuṭaṃnirudhyavāmena
pavanaṃpūrayet tatastamevacatuḥṣaṣṭivāramuccaran dakṣānāmā
kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃvāmanāsāpuṭaṃcatathaivanirudhya dvābhyāṃpavanaṃ
kuṃbhayet tatastamevadvātriṃśadvāramuccaran vāmanāsāpuṭaṃta

pṛ0 10ba) thaivanirudhya dakṣenapavanaṃpūrayet ityekaḥprāṇāyāmaḥ eṣeva
viparyayeṇakṛtodvitīyaḥ prathamavattṛtīyaḥ itthaṃprāṇāyāmatraye
ṇanirastasakalakaluṣaṃdevatārādhanayogyaṃsvaśarīraṃvibhāvya 
ṛṣyādismaraṇaṃvidadhitayathā tatrādauśrīmahāgaṇapateḥ asya
śrīmahāgaṇapatimaṃtrasya śrīvrahmāṛṣiḥ(śi) gāyatraṃchaṃdaḥ(mu) śrīmahā
gaṇapatirdevatā(hṛ) gaṃvījaṃ(gu) hrīṃśaktiḥ(pā) namaḥkīlakaṃ(nā) mamacaturvarga
phalaprāptyarthegaṇapatiprasādasiddhyarthejapeviniyogaḥ(sa0) iti 

pṛ0 11a) ṛṣyādinyāsaḥ athakaraṣaḍaṃganyāsaḥ oṃgāṃaṃguṣṭhābhyāṃnamaḥ hṛda
yāyanamaḥ gīṃtarjanībhyāṃsvāhā śirasesvāhā gūṃmadhyamābhyāṃvaṣa
ṭ śikhāyaivaṣaṭ gaiṃanāmikābhyāṃhuṃ kavacāyahuṃ gauṃkaniṣṭhi
kābhyāṃvauṣaṭ netratrayāyavauṣaṭ gaḥkaratalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃphaṭ a
strāyaphaṭ itikaraṣaḍaṃgaḥ tataḥsvahṛdayakamaleśrīmahāgaṇa
patiṃdhyāyedyathā athadhyānaṃ oṃgajavadanamaciṃtyaṃtīkṣṇadaṃtaṃtrine
traṃvṛhadudaramanaṃtaṃdaṃtamāledadhānaṃ paraśucaṣakapadmadvaṃdvahastāra

pṛ0 11ba) viṃdaṃhariyugalaniviṣṭaṃśrīgaṇeśaṃbhajāmi athagāyatrī maṃta
tpuruṣāyavidmahe vakratuṃḍāyadhīmahi tannodaṃtīpracodayāt i
mānasopacāraiḥsaṃpūjya athamaṃtraḥ hrīṃgaṃhrīṃgaṇapatayenamaḥ i
timaṃtraḥ mālāmādāya yathāśaktijapaṃvidhāya guhyātiguhya0 
ititejomayaṃjapaphalaṃdevatāvāmahastesamarpya yonimudrayāpra
ṇamet tataḥśrībhairavamaṃtreṇaprāṇāyāmaṃvidhāya asyaśrīkāme
śvarabhairavamaṃtrasya dakṣiṇāmūrtiṛṣiḥ(śi) paṃkticchaṃdaḥ(mu) śrīkāmeśvaro

pṛ0 12a) devatā(hṛ) śrīṃvījaṃ(gu) hrīṃśaktiḥ(pā) klīṃkīlakaṃ(nā) mamaśrīvidyāmaṃtrasiddha
yejapeviniyogaḥ(sa0) itiṛṣyādinyāsaḥ athakaraṣaḍaṃganyāsaḥ 
aiṃaṃguṣṭhābhyāṃnamaḥ hṛdayāyanamaḥ klīṃtarjanībhyāṃsvāhā śirasesvā
hā sauṃḥmadhyamābhyāṃvaṣaṭ śikhāyaivaṣaṭ aiṃanāmikābhyāṃhuṃ
kavacāyahuṃ klīṃkaniṣṭhikābhyāṃvauṣaṭ netratrayāyavauṣaṭ sauṃḥkara
talakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃphaṭ astrāyaphaṭ itikaraṣaḍaṃgaḥ śrīparabhairavaṃ
dhyāyedyathā athadhyānaṃ sūryakoṭisamānābhaṃcaṃdramauliṃtrilocanaṃ 

pṛ0 12ba) pāśāṃkuśadhanurvāṇāndhārayaṃtaṃśivaṃbhaje itidhyātvā māna
sopacārairārādhya athagāyatrī oṃkāmeśvarāyavidmahe śaktīśva
rāyadhīmahi tannātmāpracodayāt 3 athamaṃtraḥ aṃhrīṃśrīṃ
klīṃkāmeśvarāyanamaḥ punarapiprāṇāyāmṛṣyādikaraṣaḍaṃ
ganyāsānvidhāya guhyātiguhya itiyonimudrayātejoma
yaṃjapaphalaṃdevatāvāmahastesamarpya praṇamyaca asyaśrīma
hātripurasuṃdarīmaṃtrarājasya śrīdakṣiṇāmūrtiṛṣiḥ(śi) paṃkti-

pṛ0 13a) chaṃdaḥ(mu) śrīmahātripurasuṃdarīdevatā(hṛ) aiṃprathamakūṭaṃvījaṃ(gu) sauṃḥ
tṛtīyakūṭaṃśaktiḥ(pā) klīṃdvitīyakūṭaṃkīlakaṃ(nā) mamaśrīcaturvarga
siddhayejapeviniyogaḥ(sa0) itiṛṣyādinyāsaḥ athakaraṣaḍaṃganyā
saḥ aiṃprathamakūṭaṃaṃguṣṭhābhyāṃnamaḥ hṛdayāyanamaḥ klīṃdvitīya
kūṭaṃtarjanībhyāṃsvāhā śirasesvāhā sauṃḥtṛtīyakūṭaṃmadhyamā
bhyāṃvaṣaṭ śikhāyaivaṣaṭ aiṃprathamakūṭaṃanāmikābhyāṃhuṃ ka
vacāyahuṃ klīṃdvitīyakūṭaṃkaniṣṭhikābhyāṃvauṣaṭ netratrayāyavau

pṛ0 13ba) ṣaṭ sauṃḥtṛtīyakūṭaṃkaratalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃphaṭ astrāyaphaṭ

śrīṃtripuredeviparivārasamanvite tvāṃsnāpayāmitīrthesminmaṃ
tramūrtenamostute itimaṃtreṇatāṃsāvaraṇāṃsnapayitvā yonimu
drayāmūlenatajjalamaṣṭadhābhimaṃtrya tatradevatāsmaraṇapūrvakaṃtri
rmajjyonmajjya kuṃbhamudrayāmūlavidyayāsvaśirasitrirabhiṣicya

pṛ0 27ba) mūlenaśrīdevīṃsaṃtarpya devīṃsvahṛdisamānīya 4ṃprathamakū
ṭaṃātmatatvaṃśodhayāmisvāhā 4ṃdvitīyakūṭaṃvidyātatvaṃśodha
yāmisvāhā 4ṃtṛtīyakūṭaṃśivatatvaṃśodhayāmisvāhā ititri
rācamya tatravidhivaddhyātvā sakalīkṛtya manasāsaṃpūjya kuṃbhamu
drayāsadevatāmāvāhitāṃtrirabhiṣicya śravaṇādisaptachidrā-
ṇisaṃruddhya nirmajjyonmajet mṛtikāyāmūlenalalāṭetripuṃḍrakaṃ
kuryāt mūlenavasujalenaśodhayet tatomūlenatrairdevīṃsaṃtarpya

pṛ0 28a) tīramāruhya dhautavāsasīparidhāya vaidikasaṃdhyāṃsamāpya tāṃ

prātaḥkālemahādevividyāvāgīśvarītathā kāmeśvarīcamadhyāhne

pṛ0 28ba) sāyāhnetripureśvarī ardharātremahādevijñeyātripurasuṃdarī saṃ

pṛ0 29a) taṃdakṣabhujenidhāya mūlenatrikhaṃḍāṃvaddhvā prāṇāyāmṛṣyādi

pṛ0 29ba) raḥtriḥprokṣyā'vaśiṣṭajalaṃvāmahastenidhāya tejorūpaṃtajja

pṛ0 30a) maṃtreṇakuśaiḥsvaśirastriḥprokṣya 4ṃprathamakūṭaṃātmatattvaṃśo-

pṛ0 30ba) sāṃvālāmalasamujjvalāṃ itiprātaḥsaṃdhyā jalamaṃjalinā

pṛ0 31a) mūlaṃdevīṃtriḥsaṃtarpya śrīsūryaṃcatriḥsaṃtarpya mūlādhāreprathamaṃ

pṛ0 31ba) dinājapaṃdevyaisamarpya sūryamaṃḍalānmūladevīṃsuhṛdivahnimaṃ

pṛ0 32a) kuśābhītivarādadhānāṃ śucismitāmudbhaṭayauvanādyāṃkāme

pṛ0 32ba) athasāyaṃsaṃdhyā tatraprāṇāyāmādisūryatarpaṇāṃtaṃprātaḥ-

dhyamayoḥ 4ṃāṃnamaḥ anāmayoḥ 4ṃsauṃḥnamaḥkaniṣṭakayoḥ 4ṃaṃ
namaḥaṃguṣṭayoḥ 4ṃāṃnamaḥtarjanyoḥ 4ṃsauṃḥnamaḥkaratalakarapṛ-

pṛ0 54a) ṣṭhayoḥ itivinyasya 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥnamastripurasuṃdarimāṃrakṣa 2 
itimūrdhādipādāṃtaṃtrirvyāpakaṃvinyasya punaruccārya ūrdhvo
rdhaṃtālatrayaṃtarjanyaṃguṣṭhaśavdenadaśadigvaṃdhanaṃvidhāya 
triśūlākārayorvyastayoḥkarayoḥsvamūrdhānaṃparitastrirbhrā
maṇenavahniprākāratrayaṃcakṛtvā prāṇāyāmatrayaṃcavidhāya 
saṃvitśodhanaṃkuryāt tadvidhiryathā saṃvidāsavayormadhye
saṃvidecagarīyasī bhakṣitābhavanāśāyanirgaṃdhāvodharūpiṇī 

pṛ0 54ba) sāpunaḥsaṃvidīśānicaturdhākathitāśive vrāhmaṇīśveta
puṣpācakṣatriyāraktapuṣpikā pītapuṣpācavaiśyāvaiśūdrāca
kṛṣṇapuṣpikā ānaṃdenavinābhraṃśonacatṛpyaṃtidevatā tatra
viṃdustrikoṇavṛttaṃcacaturasraṃkārayettataḥ tasyoparioṃhrīṃ
ādhāraśaktayenamaḥ anenāsanaṃsaṃpūjyasaṃvidaṃsthāpayetsu-
dhīḥ tasyoparikaraṃdatvāmaṃtrayetsaṃvidaṃśive nyāsādikaṃtataḥ
kuryāddigvaṃdhanapuraḥsaraṃ oṃasyaśrīvijayāmaṃtrasyaśiva

pṛ0 55a) ṛṣiḥ(śi) triṣṭupchaṃdaḥ(mu) śrīvijayādevatā(hṛ) saṃvicchodhanevi-
niyogaḥ(mu) dhyānaṃ pāśāṃkuśaucāpavaraṃcavāṇaṃdorbhirdadhānāṃ
sakalārthadātrīṃ divyāṃvarāṃbhūṣaṇabhūṣitāṃcapadmāṃsanāṃ
śrīvijayāṃnamāmi itidhyātvā śodhanaṃkuryāt saṃvidebra
hmasaṃbhūtevrahmaputrisadānaghe vrāhmaṇānāṃcatṛptyarthaṃpavitrā
bhavasarvadā 4ṃoṃvrāhmaṇyainamaḥsvāhā itimaṃtreṇavrāhmīṃsaṃśo
dhya oṃsiddhamūlyakṣayedevihīnavodhapravodhini rājaprajā

pṛ0 55ba) vaśaṃkariśatrupakṣanisūdini 4ṃaiṃkṣatriyāyainamaḥsvāhā 
itimaṃtreṇakṣatriyāṃsaṃśodhya oṃajñāneṃdhanadīptāgnejñānā
gnivrahmarūpiṇi ānaṃdasyādgatiṃprītiṃsamyagjñānaṃprayaccha
me hrīṃvaiśyāyainamaḥsvāhā itimaṃtreṇavaiśyāyaisaṃśodhya oṃ
nasyāminamasyāmiyogamārgapradarśini trailokyavijayemā
taḥsamādhiphaladābhava klīṃśūdrāyainamaḥsvāhā itimaṃtreṇaśū
drāṇīṃsaṃśodhya athacaikenamaṃtreṇasarvāstāḥśodhayetsudhīḥ 

pṛ0 56a) ityabhimaṃtrya oṃamṛteamṛtodbhaveamṛtavarṣiṇiamṛtamā
karṣaya 2 siddhiṃdehiśrīmahātripurasuṃdarīpadaṃmevaśamāna
yasvāhā ititaduparisaptadhāmūlaṃcajaptvā ānaṃdeśvarabhaira
vaṃbhairavīṃcasaptadhājapet dhenumudrayāmṛtīkṛtya yoniṃprada-
rśya humityavaguṃṭhya phaḍitisaṃrakṣya mūlaṃāgaccha 2 saṃtiṣṭha 2 
sannidehi 2 sanniruddhasva 2 saṃmukhībhava mamasarvopacārasa
hitāṃpūjāṃgṛhṇa 2 svāhā ityāvāhanādimudrāḥsaṃpradarśya 

pṛ0 56ba) tālatrayeṇadivyadṛṣṭyāvāmapārṣṇighātatrayeṇaca trividha
vighnānutsārya choṭikābhiḥphaḍitidaśadigvaṃdhanaṃvidhā-
ya vāmahastakṛtatattvamudrayā ādaubhrūmadhyeānaṃdeśvaraṃbhai
ravīṃcatridhāsaṃtarpya tatośirasisvaguruṃtrivāraṃvāsaṃtarpya sva
hṛdiparadevatāṃcasaṃtarpya saṃvitsvīkāraṃkurvītayathā aiṃvada 2 
vāgvādinimamajihvāgresthirībhavasarvasattvavaśaṃkarisvāhā 
ityanenamukhejuhuyāt tatānaṃdamayobhūtvā bhūtaśuddhiṃ

pṛ0 57a) kuryādyathā oṃasyaśrībhūtaśuddhimahāmaṃtrasya puruṣṛṣiḥ(śi) 
devīgāyatrīchaṃdaḥ(mu) ākāśavāyvagnijalabhūmayodevatā(hṛ) haṃ
yaṃraṃlaṃvaṃvījāni(gu) mamapāṃcabhautikaśarīraśodhanārthejape
viniyogaḥ(sa) athakaraṣaḍaṃganyāsaḥ laṃraṃyaṃvaṃhuṃatanusūkṣmā
prabhedātmaneaṃguṣṭhābhyāṃnamaḥ hṛdayāyanamaḥ laṃraṃyaṃvaṃhaṃa-
vyaktapravodhātmanetarjanībhyāṃsvāhā śirasesvāhā laṃraṃyaṃvaṃ
haṃaciṃtyaśaktyātmanemadhyamābhyāṃvaṣaṭ śikhāyavaṣaṭ 

pṛ0 57ba) laṃraṃyaṃhaṃanaṃvidyātmaneanāmikābhyāṃhuṃ kavacāyahuṃ
laṃraṃyaṃvaṃhaṃabhedyakavacātmanekaniṣṭhikābhyāṃvauṣaṭ netratra
yāyavauṣaṭ laṃraṃyaṃvaṃhaṃaluptagatyātmanekaratalakarapṛṣṭhā
bhyāṃphaṭ astrāyaphaṭ itikaraṣaḍaṃgaḥ bhūrbhuvaḥsvaromitidi
gvaṃdhaḥ dhyānaṃ pāṃcabhautikasaṃbhūtaṃkalāpaṃcakasaṃyutaṃ vrahmā
dipaṃcasaṃyuktaṃpaṃcīkaraṇavargakaṃ paṃcapaṃcātmakamidaṃśiva
rūpaṃtanuṃbhaje itidhyānaṃ pādādijānuparyaṃtaṃpṛthvīsthānaṃ

pṛ0 58a) caturasraṃvajralāṃchitaṃpītavarṇaṃvrahmadaivataṃnivṛtikalā
dhiṣṭhitaṃlaṃvījayutaṃdhyātvā jānvādinābhiparyaṃtaṃapāṃ
sthānaṃśuklavarṇaṃardhacaṃdrākāraṃśṛṃgadvayepipadmalāṃchitaṃ
viṣṇudaivataṃpratiṣṭhākalādhiṣṭhitaṃvaṃvījayutaṃdhyātvā nā
bhyādikaṃṭhaparyaṃtaṃvahnimaṃḍalaṃsvastikopetaṃtrikoṇākāraṃ
raktavarṇaṃrudradaivataṃvidyākalādhiṣṭhitaṃraṃvījayutaṃdhyātvā 
kaṃṭhādibhrūmadhyaparyaṃtaṃvāyumaṃḍalaṃṣaṭkoṇākāraṃṣaṭviṃ

pṛ0 58ba) dulāṃchitaṃ vṛttopetaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ īśvaradaivataṃśāṃtikalādhi
ṣṭhitaṃyaṃvījayutaṃdhyātvā bhrūmadhyādivrahmaraṃdhrāṃtaṃ ākāśa
sthānaṃvṛttākāraṃdhvajalāṃchitaṃ dhūmravarṇaṃ sadāśivadaivataṃ
śāṃtyatītākalādhiṣṭhitaṃhaṃvījayutaṃdhyātvā tatra paṃcabhūta
mayedehedharmakaṃdasamudbhūtaṃjñānanālakamaiśvaryāṣṭa(aṇimā mahimā laghimā garimā āptiḥ prākāmyaṃ īśitvaṃ vaśituṃ)dalope
taṃparavairāgyakarṇikaṃsvahṛdayāraviṃdaṃdhyātvā tatkarṇikā-
yāṃjīvātmānamārāgramātraṃpradīpakalikābhāsvaramanādi

pṛ0 59a) sattāsaṃchannaparacidrūpaṃsaṃciṃtya prāguktarūpeṇamūlādhārā
tkuṃḍalinīmutthāpya suṣumṇāvartmanāmūlādhārādyanāhatāṃ
tasthavarṇādidevatāgrasaṃtīṃhṛdayakamalamānīya tayā
kavalīkṛtaṃjīvātmānaṃdhyāyan suṣumṇāvartmanāviśu-
ddhyādicakrasyavarṇādhidevatāgrasaṃtīṃbrahmaraṃdhraṃnītvā tatra
sthitasahasradalakamalakarṇikāmadhyavartiniparamātma
jyotiṣisuvarṇavarṇadevatākadaṃvakaṃjīvātmānaṃhaṃsiti 

pṛ0 59ba) maṃtreṇasaṃyojya kuṃḍalinīṃsvasthānamānīya tatvasaṃhāraṃkuryā
t tatrapṛthivīsthānepādeṃdriyagamanakriyāgaṃtavya gaṃdhaghrā
ṇapṛthivīvrahmanivṛtisamānavāyūnsaṃsmṛtya oṃhrāṃvrahmaṇe
pṛthivyadhipatayenivṛtikalātmanehuṃphaṭsvāhā itimaṃtreṇa 
tānkuṃḍalinīdvārāpāṃmadhyesaṃharet tato'pāṃsthānehastadā
nadātavyarasarasanājalaviṣṇupratiṣṭhodānātsaṃciṃtya oṃhrīṃ
viṣṇavejalādhipatayepratiṣṭhākalātmanehuṃphaṭsvāhā iti

pṛ0 60a) tānsarvānkuṃḍalinīdvārāvahnimadhyesaṃharet tatovahnisthāne
pāpuvisargavisarjanīyarūpacakṣurvahnirudravidyāvyānānsaṃsmṛ
tya oṃhrūṃrudrāyatejodhipatayevidyākalātmanehuṃphaṭsvāhā i
titānsarvānvāyumaṃḍalesaṃharet tatovāyusthāneupasthānaṃdata
dviṣayasparśaspraṣṭavyavāyvīśvaraśāṃtya'pānānsaṃsmṛtya oṃhraiṃīśvarā
yavāyvadhipatayeśāṃtikalātmanehuṃphaṭsvāhā ititānsarvā
nākāśasthānesaṃharet tata ākāśasthānevāgvacanavaktavya

pṛ0 60ba) śavdaśrotrākāśasadāśivaśāṃtyatītāprāṇānsaṃsmṛtya oṃ
hrauṃsadāśivāyākāśādhipatayeśāṃtyatītākalātmanehuṃ
phaṭsvāhā ititānsarvānkuṃḍalinyāṃsaṃhṛtya tāṃviṃduśaktau
viṃdaśaktiṃnādaśaktau nādaśaktiṃparamaśaktau praṇaveṇasaṃhṛ
tya oṃhrīṃhuṃphaṭsvāhā ititāṃparamaśaktiṃpūrvoktaparamāni
niyojya kevalaśarīrevāmakukṣaupāpapuruṣaṃciṃtayedyathā 
oṃvāmakukṣisthitaṃpāpapuruṣaṃkajjalaprabhaṃ vrahmahatyāśira-

pṛ0 61a) skaṃcasvarṇasteyabhujadvayaṃ 1 surāpānahṛdāyuktaṃgurutalpa-
kaṭidvayaṃ tatsaṃsargipadadvaṃdvamaṃgapratyaṃgapātakaṃ 2 upapāta
karomāṇaṃraktaśmaśruvilocanaṃ adhomukhaṃkṛṣṇavarṇaṃaṃgu
ṣṭhaparimāṇakaṃ samastāsurapakṣāṇāṃvardhanaṃkalmaṣātmakaṃ 
khaḍgacarmadharaṃkruddhaṃpāpaukukṣiṃviciṃtayet 4 itipāpapuruṣaṃ
viciṃtya vāmanāsāraṃdhreṇayaṃvījenasahavāyumāpūrya nābhi-
sthaṣaṭkoṇākārevāyumaṃḍalesaṃyojya tatrayamitivāyuvījaṃ

pṛ0 61ba) kṛṣṇavarṇaṃsaṃciṃtya kuṃbhakenayamitivāyuvījamāvartayaṃsta
dutthitenamahāvāyunāsvapāpapuruṣaṃsvaśarīrasthaṃsaṃśoṣya
vāyuṃdakṣiṇanāsayāvirecya punardakṣiṇanāsayāraṃvījenasa
havāyumāpūrya mūlādhāresaṃyojya tatrasthavahnimaṃḍaleraṃmi-
tivahnivījaṃraktavarṇaṃviciṃtya kuṃbhakenaramitivījamāvarta
yaṃstadutthitamahāvahnināsvapāpapuruṣaṃsvaśarīrasthaṃsaṃdahya
pāpapuruṣabhasmanāsahitaṃvāyuṃvāmanāsayāvirecya punarvā

pṛ0 62a) manāsayāvaṃvījenasahavāyumāpūryabrahmaraṃdhresaṃyojya tatra
sthaciccaṃdramaṃḍale vamityamṛtavījaṃśuklavarṇaṃviciṃtya kuṃbha
kenavaṃvījamāvartayaṃstatrasthāmṛtadhārayāsvadehaṃbhasmaplu
taṃvibhāvya taṃvāyuṃdakṣiṇanāsayāvirecya punardakṣanāsayā
laṃvījenasahavāyumāpūrya mūlādhārasthapārthivamaṃḍalesaṃ
yojya tatralamitipṛthvīvījaṃpītavarṇaṃsaṃciṃtya kuṃbhakena
laṃvījamāvartayaṃstadudbhūtenatejasāmṛtaplāvitabhasmaghanī

pṛ0 62ba) kṛtya taṃvāyuṃvāmanāsayāvirecya punarvāmanāsayāvījenavā
yumāpūrya mūlādhārasthaviṃdumadhyemāyāvījaṃsphuradvālārkava-
rṇaṃsaṃciṃtya kuṃbhakenamāyāvījamāvartayaṃstenakaracaraṇādya
vayavasametaṃsvadehaṃniṣpannaṃvibhāvya taṃvāyuṃdakṣanāsayāvire
cayet itisthūlaśarīraṃniṣpādya sūkṣmaśarīraṃniṣpādayet tatra
pūrvoktasahasrārakamalasthaparamātmanaḥsakāśātsakalajaga
tsisṛkṣāvijṛṃbhitaparavrahmecchārūpiṇīṃparaśaktiṃoṃhrīṃnamaḥ 

pṛ0 63a) itimaṃtreṇasvasthānamānīya tatonādaśaktiṃtatoviṃduśaktiṃ
praṇavenaspṛṣṭvā viṃduśakteḥsakāśātsakalajagatsṛṣṭihetubhū
tāṃkuṃḍalinīṃpraṇavenaniḥsārya tataḥoṃhrauṃsadāśivāyākā-
śādhipatayeśāṃtyatītākalātmanenamaḥ itiprāṇaśāṃtyatītā
sadāśivākāśaśrotraśavdavaktavyavacanavācākāśasthāne
sthāpayet tataḥ ākāśamaṃḍalāt oṃhraiṃīśvarāyavāyvadhipataye
śāṃtikalātmanenamaḥ itya'pānaśāṃtīśvaravāyutvaksparśaspra

pṛ0 63ba) ṣṭavyātadviṣayānaṃdopasthānvāyusthānesthāpayet tataḥoṃ
hrūṃrudrāyatejodhipatayevidyākalātmanenamaḥ itivyāna
vidyārudratejaścakṣūrūpavisarjanīyavisargapāyūnagnisthā
nesthāpayet tataḥ oṃhrīṃviṣṇavejalādhipatayepratiṣṭhākalā
tmanenamaḥ itiudānapratiṣṭhā viṣṇujalarasanārasadātavyadā
nahastānapāṃsthānesthāpayet tataḥ oṃhrāṃvrahmaṇepṛthivyadhipa
tayenivṛttikalātmanenamaḥ itisamānanivṛttivrahmapṛthivī

pṛ0 64a) ghrāṇagaṃtavyagamanakriyāpādeṃdriyāṇipṛthivīsthānesthāpa
yet tataḥpraṇavenakuṃḍalinīṃsvasthānesthāpayitvā nādaśa
ktiviṃduśaktiparamaśaktīnāṃpraṇavenasvadehevyāptiṃvibhā
vya sohamitimaṃtreṇavrahmaraṃdhrasthaparamātmanaḥsakāśātkuṃḍa
linīṃjīvātmānaṃcasaṃhṛtyavarṇavarṇādhidevatāḥsthāpayan 
hṛdayakamalamānīya hṛdayakamalejīvātmānaṃsthāpayitvā 
kuṃḍalinīṃsuṣumṇāmārgeṇamaṇipūrakādicakreṣu tattadva

pṛ0 64ba) rṇavarṇādhidevatāsthāpayanmūlādhāraṃnītvā tatrasthāpaye
t tataḥsvaśarīraṃnirastasakalakaluṣaṃdevatārādhanayogyaṃ
vibhāvya prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃkuryāt itibhūtaśuddhividhiḥ a
thaprāṇapratiṣṭhā oṃasyaśrīprāṇapratiṣṭhāmaṃtrasya śrīvra-
hmaviṣṇuśivāṛṣayaḥ(śi) ṛgyajuḥsāmānichaṃdāṃsi(mu) parāprā
ṇaśaktirdevatā(hṛ) āvījaṃ(gu) hrīṃśaktiḥ(pā) kroṃ0kīlakaṃ(nā) mamaśrīprā
ṇapratiṣṭhārtheviniyogaḥ(sa) itiṛṣyādinyāsaḥ athakaraṣaḍaṃgaḥ

pṛ0 65a) oṃāṃhrīṃkroṃaṃkaṃkhaṃgaṃghaṃṅaṃākāśavāyvagnisalilapṛthi
vyātmaneāṃaṃguṣṭhābhyāṃnamaḥ hṛdayāyanamaḥ oṃāṃhrīṃkroṃiṃ
caṃ 5ṃ śavdasparśarūparasagaṃdhātmaneīṃtarjanībhyāṃsvāhā śira
sesvāhā oṃāṃhrīṃkroṃuṃṭaṃ 5ṃ śrotratvakcakṣurjihvāghrāṇātma
neūṃmadhyamābhyāṃvaṣaṭ śikhāyaivaṣaṭ oṃāṃhrīṃkroṃeṃtaṃ 5ṃ 
vākpāṇipādapāyūpasthātmaneaiṃanāmikābhyāṃhuṃkavacā
yahuṃ oṃāṃhrīṃkroṃoṃpaṃ 5ṃ vacanādānavisargagamanānaṃdātma

pṛ0 65ba) neauṃkaniṣṭhikābhyāṃvauṣaṭ netratrayāyavauṣaṭ oṃāṃhrīṃ
kroṃaṃyaṃ 9ṃ manovuddhyahaṃkāracittātmaneaṃḥkaratalaka
ratalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃphaṭ astrāyaphaṭ itikaraṣaḍaṃganyā-
saḥ tataḥ nābhyādipādadvayāṃtaṃ āṃnamaḥhṛdādinābhyaṃtaṃ
hrīṃnamaḥmūrdhādihṛdaṃtaṃ kroṃnamaḥ hṛdayādikamalamadhye 
4ṃyaṃtvagātmanenamaḥ pūrvadale 4ṃraṃasṛgātmanenamaḥ paścime 
4ṃ laṃmāṃsātmanenamaḥ āgneye 4ṃvaṃmedasātmanenamaḥ vāyavye 

pṛ0 66a) 4ṃśaṃaṣṭyātmanenamaḥ īśāne 4ṃṣaṃmajjātmanenamaḥ naiṛte 4ṃ
saṃśukrātmanenamaḥ uttare 4ṃhaṃprāṇātmanenamaḥ dakṣiṇe 4ṃlaṃ
jīvātmanenamaḥ punaḥkarṇikāmadhye 4ṃkṣaṃḥparamātmanenamaḥ 
itivinyasyadhyāyedyathā oṃraktāvdhipotāruṇapadmasaṃsthāṃ
pāśāṃkuśāvikṣuśarāsavāṇān śūlaṃkapālaṃdadhatīṃkarāvjai
raktāṃtrinetrāṃpraṇamāmidevīṃ itidhyātvā hṛdihastaṃnidhāya 
oṃāṃhrīṃkroṃyaṃraṃlaṃvaṃśaṃṣaṃsaṃhaṃlaṃkṣaṃḥhauṃhaṃsaḥsohaṃśrīkāme

pṛ0 66ba) śvaraśivayutaśrīmahātripurasuṃdaryāḥprāṇāihaprāṇāḥ oṃ
āṃhrīṃkroṃyaṃ 9ṃhauṃhaṃsaḥsohaṃjīvihasthitaḥsarveṃdriyāṇi
ihasthitāni oṃāṃhrīṃkroṃyaṃ 9ṃ hauṃhaṃsaḥsohaṃvāṅmanastvakca
kṣurjihvāśrotraghrāṇaprāṇāihāgatyasukhaṃciraṃtiṣṭhaṃtusvāhā 
itiprāṇapratiṣṭhāṃtriḥsakṛdvājaptvā nijavapurjyotirmayaṃdhyā
yet itiprāṇapratiṣṭhā athāṃtarmātṛkānyāsaḥ tatrasvaraiḥpū

namaḥdakṣapādakakṣe 4ṃṭhaḥnamaḥjānuni 4ṃḍaḥnamaḥgulphe 4ṃḍhaḥna
maḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃṇaḥnamaḥaṃgulyagre 4ṃtaḥnamaḥvāmapādaka
kṣe 4ṃthaḥnamaḥjānuni 4ṃdaḥnamaḥgulphe 4ṃdhaḥnamaḥaṃgulimūle 
4ṃnaḥnamaḥaṃgulyagre 4ṃpaḥnamaḥdakṣapārśve 4ṃphaḥnamaḥvāme 4ṃvaḥna

pṛ0 78a) maḥpṛṣṭe 4ṃbhaḥnamaḥnābhau 4ṃmaḥnamaḥjaṭhare 4ṃyaḥtvagātmanena
maḥhṛdi 4ṃraḥasṛgātmanenamaḥdakṣāṃse 4ṃlaḥmāṃsātmanena
maḥkakudi 4ṃvaḥmedasātmanenamaḥvāmāṃse 4ṃśaḥasthyātma
nenamaḥhṛdādidakṣapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃṣaḥmajjātmanenamaḥhṛdādivā
mapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃsaḥśukrātmanenamaḥhṛdādidakṣapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃhaḥ
prāṇātmanenamaḥhṛdādivāmapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃlaḥjīvātmanenamaḥ
pādādinābhyaṃtaṃ 4ṃkṣaḥparamātmanenamaḥnābhyādimūrdhātaṃ

pṛ0 78ba) itisṛṣṭikrameṇavisargamātṛkānyāsaḥ athasthitikrame
ṇamātṛkānyāsaḥ tatrṛṣyādipūrvavat dhyānaṃyathā ḍaḥnamaḥ
dakṣapādagulphe 4ṃḍhaḥnamaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃṇaḥnamaḥaṃgulyagre 
4ṃtaḥnamaḥvāmapādakakṣe 4ṃthaḥnamaḥjānuni 4ṃdaḥnamaḥgulphe 
4ṃdhaḥnamaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃnaḥaṃgulyagre 4ṃpaḥnamaḥdakṣapā-
rśve 4ṃphaḥnamaḥvāme 4ṃvaḥnamaḥpṛṣṭe 4ṃbhaḥnamaḥnābhau 4ṃmaḥnamaḥ
jaṭhare 4ṃyaḥtvagātmanenamaḥhṛdi 4ṃraḥasṛgātmanenamaḥdakṣāṃse 

pṛ0 79a) 4ṃlaḥmāṃsātmanenamaḥkakudi 4ṃvaḥmedasātmanenamaḥvāmāṃse 
4ṃśaḥasthyātmanenamaḥhṛdādidakṣapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃṣaḥmajjātmanena
maḥhṛdādivāmapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃsaḥśukrātmanenamaḥhṛdādidakṣa-
pādāṃtaṃ 4ṃhaḥprāṇātmanenamaḥhṛdādivāmapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃlaḥjīvā
tmanenamaḥpādādinābhyaṃtaṃ 4ṃkṣaḥparamātmanenamaḥnābhyādimū-
rdhāṃtaṃ 4ṃaṃḥnamaḥśirasi 4ṃāḥnamaḥmukhavṛtte 4ṃiḥnamaḥdakṣanetre 
4ṃīḥnamaḥvāme 4ṃuḥnamaḥdakṣakarṇe 4ṃūḥnamaḥvāme 4ṃṛḥnamaḥ

pṛ0 79ba) dakṣanasi 4ṃṛḥnamaḥvāme 4ṃḷḥnamaḥdakṣagaṃḍe 4ṃḹḥnamaḥvā
me 4ṃeḥnamaḥūrdhvoṣṭe 4ṃaiḥnamaḥadhare 4ṃoḥnamaḥūrdhvadaṃteṣu 
4ṃauḥnamaḥadhodaṃteṣu 4ṃaṃḥnamaḥlalāṭe 4ṃaḥnamaḥjihvā
yāṃ 4ṃkaḥnamaḥdakṣavāhumūle 4ṃkhaḥnamaḥkurpare 4ṃgaḥnamaḥma
ṇivaṃdhe 4ṃghaḥnamaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃṅanamaḥaṃgulyagre 4ṃcaḥ
namaḥvāmavāhumūle 4ṃchaḥnamaḥkurpare 4ṃjaḥnamaḥmaṇivaṃdhe 
4ṃjhaḥnamaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃñaḥnamaḥaṃgulyagre 4ṃṭaḥnamaḥdakṣa

pṛ0 80a) pādakakṣe 4ṃṭhaḥnamaḥjānuni itisthitikrameṇavisargamā
tṛkānyāsaḥ athasthitikrameṇasaṃhāramātṛkānyāsaḥ tatra
ṛṣyādipūrvavat 4ṃkṣaḥparamātmanenamaḥhṛdādimūrdhāṃtaṃ 4ṃ
laḥjīvātmanenamaḥpādādihṛdaṃtaṃ 4ṃhaḥprāṇātmanenamaḥhṛ
dādivāmapādaṃtaṃ 4ṃsaḥśukrātmanehṛdādidakṣapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃṣaḥ
majjātmanenamaḥhṛdādivāmapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃśaḥasthyātmanenamaḥ
hṛdādidakṣapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃvaḥmedasātmanenamaḥvāmāṃse 4ṃlaḥ

pṛ0 80ba) māṃsātmanenamaḥkakudi 4ṃraḥasṛgātmanenamaḥdakṣāṃse 4ṃ
yaḥtvagātmanenamaḥhṛdi 4ṃmaḥnamaḥjaṭhare 4ṃbhaḥnamaḥnābhau 
4ṃvaḥnamaḥpṛṣṭe 4ṃphaḥnamaḥvāmapārśve 4ṃpaḥnamaḥdakṣapārśve 
4ṃnaḥnamaḥvāmapādāṃgulyagre 4ṃdhaḥnamaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃdaḥna
maḥgulphe 4ṃthaḥnamaḥjānuni 4ṃtaḥnamaḥkakṣe 4ṃṇaḥnamaḥdakṣa
pādāṃgulyagre 4ṃḍhaḥnamaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃḍaḥnamaḥgulphe 4ṃṭhaḥ
namaḥjānuni 4ṃṭaḥnamaḥkakṣe 4ṃñaḥnamaḥvāmavāhvalyagre 4ṃjhaḥ

pṛ0 81a) namaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃjaḥnamaḥmaṇivaṃdhe 4ṃchaḥnamaḥkurpare 4ṃ
caḥnamaḥvāmavāhumūle 4ṃṅaḥnamaḥdakṣavāhvagulyagre 4ṃghaḥna
maḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃgaḥnamaḥmaṇivaṃdhe 4ṃkhaḥnamaḥkurpare 4ṃ
kaḥnamaḥvāhumūle 4ṃaḥnamaḥjihvāyāṃ 4ṃaḥnamaḥlalāṭe 
4ṃauḥnamaḥadhodaṃteṣu 4ṃoḥnamaḥūrdhvadaṃteṣu 4ṃaiḥnamaḥadha
re 4ṃeḥnamaḥūrdhvoṣṭe 4ṃḹḥnamaḥvāmagaṃḍe 4ṃḷḥnamaḥdakṣagaṃḍe 
4ṃṛḥnamaḥvāmanasi 4ṃṛḥnamaḥdakṣanasi 4ṃūḥnamaḥvāmakarṇe 

pṛ0 81ba) 4ṃuḥnamaḥdakṣakarṇe 4ṃīḥnamaḥvāmanetre 4ṃiḥnamaḥdakṣanetre 4ṃ
āḥnamaḥmukhavṛtte 4ṃaḥnamaḥśirasi itisaṃhārakrameṇavi
sargamātṛkānyāsaḥ athasṛṣṭikramaviṃduvisargamātṛkānyāsaḥ 
tatrṛṣyādipūrvavat dhyānaṃyathā siṃdūrakāṃtimasitābharaṇāṃtri
netrāṃvidyākṣasūtramṛgapotavaraṃdadhānāṃ pārśvasthitāṃbhagavatīma
pikāṃcanābhāṃdhyāyetkarāvjadhṛtapustakavarṇamālāṃ 1 vāmādhaḥ
karamārabhyavāmordhvakarapaṃryatāyudhadhyānaṃ sauṃḥaṃityādikṣāṃsauṃḥ

pṛ0 82a) namaḥ itipādādimūrdhāṃtaṃmūrdhādipādāṃtaṃvinyasya 4ṃaṃḥnamaḥ
śirasi 4ṃāṃḥnamaḥmukhavṛte 4ṃiṃḥnamaḥdakṣanetre 4ṃīḥnamaḥvā
me 4ṃuṃḥnamaḥkakṣakarṇe 4ṃūṃḥnamaḥvāme 4ṃṛṃḥnamaḥdakṣanasi 
4ṃṛṃḥnamaḥvāmāyāṃ 4ṃḷṃḥnamaḥdakṣagaṃḍe 4ṃḹṃḥnamaḥvāme 4ṃeṃḥ
namaḥūrdhvoṣṭe 4ṃaiṃḥnamaḥadhare 4ṃoṃḥnamaḥūrdhvadaṃteṣu 4ṃauṃḥnamaḥ
adhodaṃteṣu 4ṃaṃḥnamaḥlalāṭe 4ṃaṃḥnamaḥjihvāyāṃ 4ṃkaṃḥnamaḥ
dakṣavāhumūle 4ṃkhaṃḥnamaḥkurpare 4ṃgaṃḥnamaḥmaṇivaṃdhe 4ṃghaṃḥnamaḥ

pṛ0 82ba) aṃgulimūle 4ṃṅaṃḥnamaḥaṃgulyagre 4ṃcaṃḥnamaḥvāmavāhumūle 
4ṃchaṃḥnamaḥkurpare 4ṃjaṃḥnamaḥmaṇivaṃdhe 4ṃjhaṃḥnamaḥaṃgulimū
le 4ṃñaṃḥnamaḥaṃgulyagre 4ṃṭaṃḥdakṣapā(da)kakṣe 4ṃṭhaṃḥjā
nuni 4ṃḍaṃḥnamaḥgulphe 4ṃḍhaṃḥnamaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃṇaṃḥnamaḥaṃ
gulyagre 4ṃtaṃḥnamaḥvāmapādakakṣe 4ṃthaṃḥnamaḥjānuni 4ṃdaṃḥnamaḥ
gulphe 4ṃdhaṃḥnamaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃnaṃḥnamaḥaṃgulyagre 4ṃpaṃḥnamaḥ
dakṣapārśce 4ṃphaṃḥnamaḥvāme 4ṃvaṃḥnamaḥpṛṣṭe 4ṃbhaṃḥnamaḥnābhau 4ṃmaṃḥ

pṛ0 82a) namaḥjaṭhare 4ṃyaṃḥtvagātmanenamaḥhṛdi 4ṃraṃḥasṛgātmanenamaḥ
dakṣāṃse 4ṃlaṃḥmāṃsātmanenamaḥkakudi 4ṃvaṃḥmedasātmanenamaḥ
vāme 4ṃśaṃḥasthyātmanenamaḥhṛdādidakṣapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃṣaṃḥmajjā
tmanenamaḥhṛdādivāmapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃsaṃḥśukrātmanenamaḥhṛdādi
dakṣapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃhaṃḥprāṇātmanenamaḥhṛdādivāmapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃlaṃḥ
jīvātmanenamaḥpādādinābhyaṃtaṃ 4ṃkṣaṃḥparamātmanenamaḥnābhyā
dimūrdhāṃtaṃ itisṛṣṭikrameṇaviṃduvisargamātṛkāmāsaḥ atha

pṛ0 83ba) sthitikrameṇaviṃduvisargamātṛkānyāsaḥ tatrṛṣyādipūrva-
vat dhyānaṃyathā oṃmṛgavālaṃvaraṃvidyāmakṣasūtraṃdadhatkaraiḥ mā
lāvidyālasaddhastāṃsadādhyeyāṃśivāṃgiraṃ evaṃdhyātvā 4ṃḍaṃḥna
maḥgulphe 4ṃḍhaṃḥnamaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃṇaṃḥnamaḥaṃgulyagre 4ṃtaṃḥ
namaḥvāmapādakakṣe 4ṃthaṃḥnamaḥjānuni 4ṃdaṃḥnamaḥgulphe 4ṃdhaṃḥ
namaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃnaṃḥnamaḥaṃgulyagre 4ṃpaṃḥnamaḥdakṣapārśve 
4ṃphaṃḥnamaḥvāme 4ṃvaṃḥnamaḥpṛṣṭe 4ṃbhaṃḥnamaḥnābhau 4ṃmaṃḥnamaḥjaṭhare 

pṛ0 84a) 4ṃyaṃḥtvagātmanenamaḥhṛdi 4ṃraṃḥasṛgātmanenamaḥdakṣāṃse 
4ṃlaṃḥmāṃsātmanenamaḥkakudi 4ṃvaṃḥmedasātmanenamaḥvāṃmā
se 4ṃśaṃḥasthyātmanenamaḥhṛdādidakṣapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃṣaṃḥmajjātma
nenamaḥhṛdādivāmapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃsaḥśukrātmanenamaḥhṛdādi
dakṣapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃhaṃḥprāṇātmanenamaḥhṛdādivāmapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃlaṃḥ

pṛ0 84ba) kṣanetre 4ṃīṃḥnamaḥvāme 4ṃuṃḥnamaḥdakṣakarṇe 4ṃūṃḥnamaḥvāme 
4ṃṛṃḥnamaḥdakṣanasi 4ṃṛṃḥnamaḥvāme 4ṃḷṃḥnamaḥdakṣagaṃḍe 4ṃ
ḹṃḥnamaḥvāme 4ṃeṃḥnamaḥūrdhvoṣṭe 4ṃaiṃḥnamaḥadhare 4ṃoṃḥna

pṛ0 85a) maṇivaṃdhe 4ṃjhaṃḥnamaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃñaṃḥnamaḥaṃgulyagre 
4ṃṭaṃḥnamaḥdakṣapādakakṣe 4ṃṭhaṃḥnamaḥjānuni itisthiti
krameṇaviṃduvisarganyāsaḥ athasaṃhārakrameṇaviṃduvisa
rganyāsaḥ tatrṛṣyādipūrvavaddhyānaṃyathā 4ṃkṣaṃḥparamātma
nenamaḥhṛdādimūrdhāṃtaṃ 4ṃlaṃḥjīvātmanenamaḥpādādihṛdaṃ
taṃ 4ṃhaṃḥprāṇātmanehṛdādivāmapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃsaṃḥśukrātmane
namaḥhṛdādidakṣapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃṣaṃḥmajjātmanenamaḥhṛdādivā

pṛ0 85ba) mapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃśaṃḥasthyātmanenamaḥhṛdādidakṣapāṇyaṃtaṃ 
4ṃvaṃḥmedasātmanenamaḥvāmāṃse 4ṃlaṃḥmāṃsātmanenamaḥ
kakudi 4ṃraṃḥasṛgātmanenamaḥdakṣāṃse 4ṃyaṃḥtvagātmanena

pṛ0 86a) gre 4ṃḍhaṃḥnamaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃḍaṃḥnamaḥgulphe 4ṃṭhaṃḥnamaḥ
jānuni 4ṃṭaṃḥnamaḥkakṣe 4ṃñaṃḥnamaḥvāmavāhvaṃgulyagre 
4ṃjhaṃḥnamaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃjaṃḥnamaḥmaṇivaṃdhe 4ṃchaṃḥnamaḥ
kurpare 4ṃcaṃḥnamaḥvāhumūle 4ṃṅaṃḥnamaḥdakṣavāhvaṃgulyagre 
4ṃghaṃḥnamaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃgaṃḥnamaḥmaṇivaṃdhe 4ṃkhaṃḥnamaḥku

pṛ0 86ba) adhare 4ṃeṃḥnamaḥūrdhvoṣṭe 4ṃḹṃḥnamaḥvāmagaṃḍe 4ṃḷṃḥnamaḥ
vāmagaṃḍe 4ṃṛṃḥnamaḥvāmanasi 4ṃṛṃḥnamaḥdakṣanasi 4ṃūṃḥ
namaḥvāmakarṇe 4ṃuṃḥnamaḥdakṣakarṇe 4ṃīṃḥnamaḥvāmanetre 4ṃ
iṃḥnamaḥdakṣanetre 4ṃāṃḥnamaḥmukhavṛtte 4ṃaṃḥnamaḥśirasi i
tisaṃhārakramaviṃduvisarganyāsaḥ athasṛṣṭikramahṛllekhādi
nyāsaḥ tatraprāṇāyāmaṃmāyāvījenapūrvavatkuryāt asya
śrīhṛllekhādimātṛkānyāsasya śaktiṛṣiḥ(śi) gāyatraṃchaṃdaḥ(mu)

pṛ0 87a) hṛllekhārūpiṇīsarasvatīdevatā(hṛ) śrīvidyāṃgatvenanyāsevi
niyogaḥ(sa) itiṛṣyādinyāsaḥ athakaraṣaḍaṃganyāsaḥ 4ṃ
hrīṃaṃkaṃkhaṃgaṃghaṃṅaṃāṃhrīṃaṃguṣṭābhyāṃnamaḥ hṛdayāyanamaḥ 4ṃ
hrīṃiṃcaṃchaṃjaṃjhaṃñaṃīṃhrīṃtarjanībhyāṃsvāhā śirasesvāhā 4ṃhrīṃ
uṃṭaṃṭhaṃḍaṃḍhaṃṇaṃūṃhrīṃmadhyāmābhyāṃvauṣaṭ śikhāyaivauṣaṭ 4ṃhrīṃeṃ
taṃthaṃdaṃdhaṃnaṃaiṃhrīṃanāmikābhyāṃhuṃ kavacāyahuṃ 4ṃoṃpaṃphaṃvaṃbhaṃ
maṃauṃhrīṃkaniṣṭhikābhyāṃvaṣaṭ 4ṃhrīṃaṃyaṃraṃlaṃvaṃśaṃṣaṃsaṃhaṃlaṃ

pṛ0 87ba) kṣaṃḥaṃḥhrīṃkaratalakarapṛṣṭābhyāṃphaṭ astrāyaphaṭ iti
karaṣaḍaṃganyāsaḥ athadhyānaṃ udyatkoṭidivākarapratibha
vāṃtuṃgorupīnastanīṃvaddhāṃdhedukirīṭahāraraśanāmaṃjīra
saṃśobhitāṃ vibhrāṇāṃkarapaṃkajairjapavaṭīṃpāśāṃkuśaupu-
stakaṃdhyāyāmojagadaṃvikāṃtrinayanāṃpadmasthitāṃsaukhyadām 
itidhyānaṃ 4ṃhrīṃaṃnamaḥhrīṃśirasi 4ṃhrīṃāṃnamaḥhrīṃmukhavṛ
tte 4ṃhrīṃiṃnamaḥhrīṃdakṣanetre 4ṃhrīṃīṃnamaḥhrīṃvāmanetre 4ṃhrīṃuṃ

pṛ0 88a) namaḥdakṣakarṇe 4ṃhrīṃūṃnamaḥvāme 4ṃhrīṃṛṃnamaḥdakṣanasi 
4ṃhrīṃṛṃnamaḥ vāmāyāṃ 4ṃhrīṃḷṃnamaḥ dakṣagaṃḍe 4ṃhrīṃḹṃnamaḥ
vāme 4ṃhrīṃeṃnamaḥ ūrdhvoṣṭe 4ṃhrīṃaiṃnamaḥ adhare 4ṃhrīṃoṃnamaḥū
rdhvadaṃteṣu 4ṃhrīṃauṃnamaḥ adhodaṃteṣu 4ṃhrīṃaṃnamaḥ lalāṭe 4ṃ
hrīṃaṃḥnamaḥjihvāyāṃ 4ṃhrīṃkaṃnamaḥdakṣavāhumūle 4ṃhrīṃkhaṃna
maḥkurpare 4ṃhrīṃgaṃnamaḥmaṇivaṃdhe 4ṃhrīṃghaṃnamaḥaṃgulimūle

pṛ0 98a) hrīṃśrīṃklīṃpādādinābhyaṃtaṃ 4ṃhrīṃśrīṃklīṃkṣaṃparamātmanenamaḥhrīṃ
śrīṃklīṃnābhyādimūrdhāṃtam ititrivījādimātṛkānyāsaḥ || 
asyaśrīvālāsaṃpuṭitamātṛkānyāsasya dakṣiṇāmūrtiṛṣiḥ(śi) 
gāyatraṃchaṃdaḥ(mu) śrīvālārūpiṇīmātṛkādevatā(hṛ) śrīvidyāṃgatve
nyāseviniyogaḥ(sa) itiṛṣyādinyāsaḥ athakaraṣaḍaṃganyāsaḥ 
aiṃaṃaṃguṣṭābhyāṃnamaḥ hṛdayāyanamaḥ klīṃtarjanībhyāṃsvāhā śirasesvā
hā sauṃḥmadhyamābhyāṃvaṣaṭ śikhāyaivaṣaṭ aiṃanāmikābhyāṃhuṃ 

pṛ0 98ba) kavacāyahuṃ klīṃkaniṣṭikābhyāṃvauṣaṭ netratrayāyavauṣaṭ sauṃḥ
karatalakarapṛṣṭābhyāṃphaṭ itikaraṣaḍaṃganyāsaḥ athadhyānaṃ oṃ
raktāṃvarācaṃdrakalāvataṃsāṃsamudyadādityanibhāṃtrinetrāṃ akṣa
srajaṃpustakābhītiyuktāṃvaraṃdadhānāṃtripurāṃbhajāmaḥ itidhyātvā 
4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃśirasi 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥāṃsauṃḥklīṃaiṃ
namaḥmukhavṛtte 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥiṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃdakṣanetre 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥ
īṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃvāme 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥuṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃ dakṣakarṇe 

pṛ0 99a) 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥūṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃvāme 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥṛṃnamaḥsauṃḥ
klīṃaiṃdakṣanasi 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥṛṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃvāmāyāṃ 4ṃaiṃ
klīṃsauṃḥḷṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃdakṣagaṃḍe 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥḹṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃ
aiṃvāme 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥeṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃūrdhvoṣṭhe 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥaiṃnamaḥ 
sauṃḥklīṃaiṃadhare 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥoṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃūrdhvadaṃteṣu 4ṃaiṃklīṃ
sauṃḥauṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃadhodaṃteṣu 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃ
lalāṭe 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥaṃḥnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃjihvāyāṃ 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥkaṃ

pṛ0 99ba) namaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃdakṣavāhumūle 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥkhaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃ
aiṃkurpare 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥgaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃmaṇivaṃdhe 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥghaṃ
namaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃaṃgulimūle 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥṅaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃaṃgu
lyagre 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥcaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃvāmavāhumūle 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥchaṃ
namaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃkurpare 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥjaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃmaṇivaṃdhe 4ṃaiṃ
klīṃsauṃḥjhaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃaṃgulimūle 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥñaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃ
aṃgulyagre 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥṭaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃdakṣapādakakṣe 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥ

pṛ0 100a) ṭhaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃjānuni 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥḍaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃgulphe 
4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥḍhaṃḥnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃaṃgulimūle 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥṇaṃnamaḥ
sauṃḥklīṃaiṃaṃgulyagre 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥtaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃvāmapādakakṣe 
4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥthaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃjānuni 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥdaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃ
aiṃgulphe 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥdhaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃaṃgulimūle 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥ
naṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃaṃgulyagre 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥpaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃdakṣapā
rśve 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥphaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃvāme 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥvaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃ

pṛ0 100ba) aiṃpṛṣṭhe 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥbhaṃnamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃnābhau 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥmaṃ
namaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃjaṭhare 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥyaṃtvagātmanenamaḥsauṃḥklīṃ
aiṃhṛdi 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥraṃasṛgātmanenamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃdakṣāṃse 
4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥlaṃmāṃsātmanenamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃkakudi 4ṃaiṃklīṃ
sauṃḥvaṃmedamātmanenamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃvāmāṃse 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥśaṃ
asthyātmanenamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃhṛdādidakṣapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥ
ṣaṃmajjātmanenamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃhṛdādivāmapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥ

pṛ0 101a) saṃśukrātmanenamaḥhṛdādidakṣapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥhaṃprāṇā
tmanenamaḥ sauṃḥklīṃaiṃhṛdādivāmapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥlaṃjī-
vātmanenamaḥsauṃḥklīṃaiṃpādādinābhyaṃtaṃ 4ṃaiṃklīṃsauṃḥkṣaṃparamā
tmanenamaḥnābhyādimūrdhāṃtaṃ itiśrīvālāsaṃpuṭitamātṛkānyā
saḥ oṃparāsaṃpuṭitamātṛkānyāsasya vrahmāṛṣiḥ gāyatraṃchaṃdaḥ
parārūpiṇīmātṛkādevatā śrīvidyāṃgatvenyāseviniyogaḥ 
itiṛṣyādinyāsaḥ athakaraṣaḍaṃganyāsaḥ sāṃaṃguṣṭābhyāṃnamaḥ 

pṛ0 101ba) hṛdayāyanamaḥ sīṃtarjanībhyāṃsvāhā śirasesvāhā sūṃma-
dhyābhyāṃvaṣaṭ śikhāyaivaṣaṭ saiṃanāmikābhyāṃhuṃ kava
cāyahuṃ sauṃkaniṣṭikābhyāṃvauṣaṭ netratrayāyavauṣaṭ saḥ
karatalakarapṛṣṭābhyāṃphaṭ astrāyaphaṭ itikaraṣaḍaṃgaḥ 
athadhyānaṃ akalaṃkaśaśāṃkābhāṃtrṛṃkṣīcaṃdrakalāvatīṃ mudrā
pustakamadvāhuṃpraṇamāmiparāṃkalāṃ itidhyātvā 4ṃsauṃḥaṃna
maḥsauṃḥśirasi 4ṃsauṃḥāṃnamaḥsauṃḥmukhavṛtte 4ṃsauṃḥiṃnamaḥ

pṛ0 102a) sauṃḥdakṣanetre 4ṃsauṃḥīṃnamaḥsauṃḥvāme 4ṃsauṃḥuṃnamaḥsauṃḥdakṣaka-
rṇe 4ṃsauṃḥūṃnamaḥsauṃḥvāme 4ṃsauṃḥṛṃnamaḥsauṃḥdakṣanasi 4ṃ
sauṃḥṛṃnamaḥsauṃḥvāmāyāṃ 4ṃsauṃḥḷṃnamaḥsauṃḥdakṣagaṃḍe 4ṃsauṃḥḹṃ
namaḥsauṃḥvāme 4ṃsauṃḥeṃnamaḥsauṃḥūrdhvoṣṭe 4ṃsauṃḥaiṃnamaḥsauṃḥa
dhare 4ṃsauṃḥoṃnamaḥsauṃḥūrdhvadaṃteṣu 4ṃsauṃḥauṃnamaḥ sauṃḥadho-
daṃteṣu 4ṃsauṃḥaṃnamaḥsauṃḥlalāṭe 4ṃsauṃḥaṃḥnamaḥsauṃḥjihvā
yāṃ 4ṃsauṃḥkaṃnamaḥsauṃḥdakṣavāhumūle 4ṃsauṃḥkhaṃnamaḥsauṃḥku

pṛ0 102ba) rpare 4ṃsauṃḥgaṃnamaḥsauṃḥmaṇivaṃdhe 4ṃsauṃḥghaṃnamaḥsauṃḥaṃguli
mūle 4ṃsauṃḥṅaṃnamaḥsauṃḥaṃgulyagre 4ṃsauṃḥcaṃnamaḥsauṃḥvāma
vāhumūle 4ṃsauṃḥchaṃnamaḥsauṃḥkurpare 4ṃsauṃḥjaṃnamaḥsauṃḥmaṇi
baṃdhe 4ṃsauṃḥjhaṃnamaḥsauṃḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃsauṃḥñaṃnamaḥsauṃḥaṃgu
lyagre 4ṃsauṃḥṭaṃnamaḥsauṃḥdakṣapādakakṣe 4ṃsauṃḥṭhaṃnamaḥsauṃḥjā
nuni 4ṃsauṃḥḍaṃnamaḥsauṃḥgulphe 4ṃsauṃḥḍhaṃnamaḥsauṃḥaṃgulimū
le 4ṃsauṃḥṇaṃnamaḥsauṃḥaṃgulyagre 4ṃsauṃḥtaṃnamaḥsauṃḥvāmapādakakṣe 

pṛ0 103a) 4ṃsauṃḥthaṃnamaḥsauṃḥjānuni 4ṃsauṃḥdaṃnamaḥsauṃḥgulphe 4ṃsauṃḥ
dhaṃnamaḥsauṃḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃsauṃḥnaṃnamaḥsauṃḥaṃgulyagre 4ṃsauṃḥ
paṃnamaḥsauṃḥdakṣapārśce 4ṃsauṃḥphaṃnamaḥsauṃḥvāme 4ṃsauṃḥvaṃnamaḥ
sauṃḥpṛṣṭhe 4ṃsauṃḥbhaṃnamaḥsauṃḥnābhau 4ṃsauṃḥmaṃnamaḥsauṃḥjaṭhare 4ṃ
sauṃḥyaṃtvagātmanenamaḥsauṃḥhṛdi 4ṃsauṃḥraṃasṛgātmanenamaḥsauṃḥ
dakṣāṃse 4ṃsauṃḥlaṃmāṃsātmanenamaḥsauṃḥkakudi 4ṃsauṃḥvaṃmedasā
tmanenamaḥsauṃḥvāmāṃse 4ṃsauṃḥśaṃasthyātmanenamaḥsauṃḥhṛdādi

pṛ0 103ba) dakṣapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃsauṃḥṣaṃmajjātmanenamaḥsauṃḥhṛdādivāmapāṇyaṃ
taṃ 4ṃsauṃḥsaṃśukrātmanenamaḥsauṃḥhṛdādidakṣapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃsauṃḥ
haṃprāṇātmanenamaḥsauṃḥhṛdādivāmapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃsauṃḥlaṃjīvā
tmanenamaḥsauṃḥhṛdādinābhyaṃtaṃ 4ṃsauṃḥkṣaṃparamātmanenamaḥsauṃḥ
nābhyādimūrdhāṃtaṃ itiparāsaṃpuṭitamātṛkānyāsaḥ athavā
gbhavasaṃpuṭitamātṛkānyāsaḥ pūrvavatṛṣyādikaraṣaṃḍaganyā
saṃkuryāt 4ṃaiṃaṃaiṃnamaḥśirasi 4ṃaiṃāṃaiṃnamaḥmukhavṛtte 

pṛ0 104a) 4ṃaiṃiṃnamaḥaiṃdakṣanetre 4ṃaiṃīṃnamaḥaiṃvāme 4ṃaiṃuṃnamaḥaiṃdakṣa
karṇe 4ṃaiṃūṃnamaḥaiṃvāme 4ṃaiṃṛṃnamaḥaiṃdakṣanasi 4ṃaiṃṛṃna
maḥaiṃvāmāyāṃ 4ṃaiṃḷṃnamaḥaiṃdakṣagaṃḍe 4ṃaiṃḹṃnamaḥaiṃvāme 4ṃ
aiṃeṃnamaḥaiṃūrdhvoṣṭe 4ṃaiṃaiṃnamaḥaiṃadhare 4ṃaiṃoṃnamaḥaiṃūrdhvadaṃ
teṣu 4ṃaiṃauṃnamaḥaiṃadhodaṃteṣu 4ṃaiṃaṃnamaḥaiṃlalāṭe 4ṃaiṃaṃḥna
maḥaiṃjihvāyāṃ 4ṃaiṃkaṃnamaḥaiṃdakṣavāhumūle 4ṃaiṃkhaṃnamaḥaiṃkurpare 
4ṃaiṃgaṃnamaḥaiṃmaṇivaṃdhe 4ṃaiṃghaṃnamaḥaiṃaṃgulimūle 4ṃaiṃṅaṃnamaḥ 

pṛ0 104ba) aiṃaṃgulyagre 4ṃaiṃcaṃnamaḥaiṃvāmavāhumūle 4ṃaiṃchaṃnamaḥaiṃku
rpare 4ṃaiṃjaṃnamaḥaiṃmaṇivaṃdhe 4ṃaiṃjhaṃnamaḥaiṃaṃgulimūle 4ṃ
aiṃñaṃnamaḥaiṃaṃgulyagre 4ṃaiṃṭaṃnamaḥaiṃdakṣapādakakṣe 4ṃaiṃṭhaṃna-
maḥaiṃjānuni 4ṃaiṃḍaṃnamaḥaiṃgulphe 4ṃaiṃḍhaṃnamaḥaiṃaṃgulimū
le 4ṃaiṃṇaṃnamaḥaiṃaṃgulyagre 4ṃaiṃtaṃnamaḥaiṃvāmapādakakṣe 4ṃaiṃ
thaṃnamaḥaiṃjānuni 4ṃaiṃdaṃnamaḥaiṃgulphe 4ṃaiṃdhaṃnamaḥaiṃaṃguli
mūle 4ṃaiṃnaṃnamaḥaiṃaṃgulyagre 4ṃaiṃpaṃnamaḥaiṃdakṣapārśve

pṛ0 105a) 4ṃaiṃphaṃnamaḥaiṃvāme 4ṃaiṃvaṃnamaḥaiṃpṛṣṭe 4ṃaiṃbhaṃnamaḥaiṃnā
bhau 4ṃaiṃmaṃnamaḥaiṃjaṭhare 4ṃaiṃyaṃtvagātmanenamaḥaiṃhṛdi 4ṃ
aiṃraṃasṛgātmanenamaḥaiṃdakṣāṃse 4ṃaiṃlaṃmāṃsātmanenamaḥaiṃ
kakudi 4ṃaiṃvaṃmedasātmanenamaḥaiṃvāmāṃse 4ṃaiṃśaṃasthyā
tmanenamaḥaiṃhṛdādidakṣapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃaiṃṣaṃmajjātmanenamaḥaiṃ
hṛdādivāmapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃaiṃsaṃśukrātmanenamaḥaiṃhṛdādidakṣa
pādāṃtaṃ 4ṃaiṃhaṃprāṇātmanenamaḥaiṃhṛdādivāmapādāṃtaṃ 

pṛ0 105ba) 4ṃaiṃlaṃjīvātmanenamaḥaiṃpādādinābhyaṃtaṃ 4ṃaiṃkṣaṃparamā-
tmanenamaḥaiṃnābhyādimūrdhāṃtaṃ itivāgbhavapuṭitamātṛkā
nyāsaḥ athamāyāpuṭitamātṛkānyāsaḥ pūrvavatṛṣyādi
karaṣaḍaṃganyāsaṃkuryāt 4ṃhrīṃaṃnamaḥhrīṃśirasi 4ṃhrīṃāṃ
namaḥhrīṃmukhavṛtte 4ṃhrīṃiṃnamaḥhrīṃdakṣanetre 4ṃhrīṃīṃnamaḥhrīṃvā
me 4ṃhrīṃuṃnamaḥhrīṃdakṣakarṇe 4ṃhrīṃūṃnamaḥhrīṃvāme 4ṃhrīṃṛṃna
maḥdakṣanasi 4ṃhrīṃṛṃnamaḥhrīṃvāmāyāṃ 4ṃhrīṃḷṃnamaḥhṛīṃdakṣagaṃḍe 

pṛ0 106a) 4ṃhrīṃḹṃnamaḥaiṃvāme 4ṃhrīṃeṃnamaḥhrīṃūrdhvoṣṭe 4ṃhrīṃaiṃnamaḥ
hrīṃadhare 4ṃhrīṃoṃnamaḥhrīṃūrdhvadaṃteṣu 4ṃhrīṃauṃnamaḥhrīṃadho
daṃteṣu 4ṃhrīṃaṃnamaḥhrīṃlalāṭe 4ṃhrīṃaṃḥnamaḥhrīṃjihvāyāṃ 4ṃ
hrīṃkaṃnamaḥdakṣavāhumūle 4ṃhrīṃkhaṃnamaḥhrīṃkurpare 4ṃhrīṃgaṃnamaḥhrīṃ
maṇivaṃdhe 4ṃhrīṃghaṃnamaḥhrīṃaṃgulimūle 4ṃhrīṃṅaṃnamaḥhrīṃaṃgu
lyagre 4ṃhrīṃcaṃnamaḥhrīṃvāmavāhumūle 4ṃhrīṃchaṃnamaḥhrīṃkurpare 
4ṃhrīṃjaṃnamaḥhrīṃmaṇivaṃdhe 4ṃhrīṃjhaṃnamaḥhrīṃaṃgulimūle 4ṃ

pṛ0 106ba) hrīṃñaṃnamaḥhrīṃaṃgulyagre 4ṃhrīṃṭaṃnamaḥhrīṃdakṣapādakakṣe 4ṃ
hrīṃṭhaṃnamaḥhrīṃjānuni hrīṃḍaṃnamaḥhrīṃgulphe 4ṃhrīṃḍhaṃnamaḥhrīṃ
aṃgulimūle 4ṃhrīṃṇaṃnamaḥhrīṃaṃgulyagre 4ṃhrīṃtaṃnamaḥhrīṃvā
mapādakakṣe 4ṃhrīṃthaṃnamaḥhrīṃjānuni 4ṃhrīṃdaṃnamaḥhrīṃgulphe 
4ṃhrīṃdhaṃnamaḥaṃgulimūle 4ṃhrīṃnaṃnamaḥhrīṃaṃgulyagre 4ṃhrīṃpaṃ
namaḥhrīṃdakṣapārśve 4ṃhrīṃphaṃnamaḥhrīṃvāme 4ṃhrīṃvaṃnamaḥhrīṃpṛṣṭhe 
4ṃhrīṃbhaṃnamaḥhrīṃnābhau 4ṃhrīṃmaṃnamaḥhrīṃjaṭhare 4ṃhrīṃyaṃtvagātma

pṛ0 107a) nenamaḥhrīṃhṛdi 4ṃhrīṃraṃasṛgātmanenamaḥhrīṃdakṣāṃse 4ṃhrīṃlaṃ
māṃsātmanenamaḥhrīṃkakudi 4ṃhrīṃvaṃmedasātmanenamaḥhrīṃvāmāṃse 4ṃhrīṃśaṃa
sthyātmanenamaḥhrīṃhṛdādidakṣapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃhrīṃṣaṃmajjātmanenamaḥ
hrīṃhṛdādivāmapāṇyaṃtaṃ 4ṃhrīṃsaṃśukrātmanenamaḥhrīṃhṛdādi
dakṣapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃhrīṃhaṃprāṇātmanenamaḥhrīṃhṛdādivāmapādāṃtaṃ 4ṃ
hrīṃlaṃjīvātmanenamaḥhrīṃpādādinābhyaṃtaṃ 4ṃhrīṃkṣaṃparamātmanenamaḥ
hrīṃnābhyādimūrdhāṃtaṃ itimāyāpuṭitaṃmātṛkānyāsaḥ ||

pṛ0 160a) oṃvaṃoṃvaṃvāmāṃse oṃśaṃoṃśaṃoṃśaṃnamaḥhṛdādidakṣapāṇyaṃtaṃ

pṛ0 160ba) śrīṃṛṃśrīṃṛṃśrīṃṛṃnamaḥdakṣanāsāpuṭe śrīṃṛṃśrīṃṛṃśrīṃṛṃnamaḥvāme

pṛ0 161a) ṅaṃśrīṃṅaṃśrīṃṅaṃnamaḥdakṣāṃgulyagre śrīṃcaṃśrīṃcaṃśrīṃcaṃnamaḥvāmavāhumū

pṛ0 161ba) namaḥaṃgulimūle śrīṃnaṃśrīṃnaṃśrīṃnaṃnamaḥaṃgulyagre śrīṃpaṃśrīṃpaṃ

namaḥpṛṣṭe śrīṃbhaṃśrīṃbhaṃśrīṃbhaṃnamaḥnābhau śrīṃmaṃśrīṃmaṃśrīṃmaṃnamaḥja

śrīṃlaṃśrīṃlaṃnamaḥkakudi śrīṃvaṃśrīṃvaṃśrīṃvaṃnamaḥvāmāṃse śrīṃśaṃśrīṃ
śaṃśrīṃśaṃnamaḥhṛdādidakṣapāṇyaṃtaṃ śrīṃṣaṃśrīṃṣaṃśrīṃṣaṃnamaḥhṛdādi
vāmapāṇyaṃtaṃ śrīṃsaṃśrīṃsaṃśrīṃsaṃnamaḥhṛdādidakṣapādāṃtaṃ śrīṃhaṃśrīṃ

pṛ0 162a) haṃśrīṃhaṃnamaḥhṛdādivāmapāṇyaṃtaṃ śrīṃlaṃśrīṃlaṃśrīṃlaṃnamaḥpādādi
nābhyaṃtaṃ śrīṃkṣaṃśrīṃkṣaṃśrīṃkṣaṃnamaḥnābhyādimūrdhāṃtaṃ itidvitīyonyā
saḥ klīṃaṃklīṃaṃklīṃaṃnamaḥśirasi klīṃāṃklīṃāṃklīṃāṃnamaḥmukhe 
klīṃiṃklīṃiṃklīṃiṃnamaḥdakṣanetre klīṃīṃklīṃīṃklīṃīṃnamaḥvāme klīṃuṃklīṃ
uṃklīṃuṃnamaḥdakṣakarṇe klīṃūṃklīṃūṃklīṃūṃnamaḥvāme klīṃṛṃklīṃṛṃklīṃ
ṛṃnamaḥdakṣanāsāpuṭe klīṃṛṃklīṃṛṃklīṃṛṃnamaḥvāme klīṃḷṃklīṃḷṃklīṃ
ḷṃnamaḥdakṣagaṃḍe klīṃḹṃklīṃḹṃklīṃḹṃnamaḥvāme klīṃeṃklīṃeṃklīṃeṃnamaḥ

pṛ0 162ba) ūrdhvoṣṭe klīṃaiṃklīṃaiṃklīṃaiṃnamaḥadharoṣṭe klīṃoṃklīṃoṃklīṃoṃnamaḥ
ūrdhvadaṃteṣu klīṃauṃklīṃauṃklīṃauṃnamaḥadhodaṃteṣu klīṃaṃklīṃaṃklīṃ
aṃnamaḥlalāṭe klīṃaṃḥklīṃaṃḥklīṃaṃḥnamaḥjihvāyāṃ klīṃkaṃklīṃkaṃ

klīṃgaṃklīṃgaṃnamaḥmaṇivaṃdhe klīṃghaṃklīṃghaṃklīṃghaṃnamaḥaṃgulimūle klīṃ
ṅaṃklīṃṅaṃklīṃṅaṃnamaḥaṃgulyagre klīṃcaṃklīṃcaṃklīṃcaṃnamaḥvāmavāhumūle

pṛ0 163a) klīṃjhaṃklīṃjhaṃklīṃjhaṃnamaḥaṃgulimūle klīṃñaṃklīṃñaṃklīṃñaṃnamaḥaṃgu
lyagre klīṃṭaṃklīṃṭaṃklīṃṭaṃnamaḥdakṣapādamūle klīṃṭhaṃklīṃṭhaṃklīṃṭhaṃnamaḥ
jānuni klīṃḍaṃklīṃḍaṃklīṃḍaṃnamaḥgulphe klīṃḍhaṃklīṃḍhaṃklīṃḍhaṃnamaḥaṃgu
limūle klīṃṇaṃklīṃṇaṃklīṃṇaṃnamaḥaṃgulyagre klīṃtaṃklīṃtaṃklīṃtaṃnamaḥ
vāmapādamūle klīṃthaṃklīṃthaṃklīṃthaṃnamaḥjānuni klīṃdaṃklīṃdaṃklīṃdaṃna
maḥgulphe klīṃdhaṃklīṃdhaṃklīṃdhaṃnamaḥaṃgulimūle klīṃnaṃklīṃnaṃklīṃnaṃna
maḥaṃgulyagre klīṃpaṃklīṃpaṃklīṃpaṃnamaḥdakṣapārśve klīṃphaṃklīṃphaṃklīṃphaṃ

pṛ0 163ba) namaḥvāmapārśve klīṃvaṃklīṃvaṃklīṃvaṃnamaḥpṛṣṭe klīṃbhaṃklīṃbhaṃklīṃbhaṃna
maḥnābhau klīṃmaṃklīṃmaṃklīṃmaṃnamaḥjaṭhare klīṃyaṃklīṃyaṃklīṃyaṃnamaḥhṛ
di klīṃraṃklīṃraṃklīṃraṃnamaḥdakṣāṃse klīṃlaṃklīṃlaṃklīṃlaṃnamaḥkaku-
di klīṃvaṃklīṃvaṃklīṃvaṃnamaḥvāmāṃse klīṃśaṃklīṃśaṃklīṃśaṃnamaḥhṛdādi
dakṣapāṇyaṃtaṃ klīṃṣaṃklīṃṣaṃklīṃṣaṃnamaḥhṛdādivāmapāṇyaṃtaṃ klīṃsaṃ
klīṃsaṃklīṃsaṃnamaḥhṛdādidakṣapādāṃtaṃ klīṃhaṃklīṃhaṃklīṃhaṃnamaḥhṛ
dādivāmapādāṃtaṃ klīṃlaṃklīṃlaṃklīṃlaṃnamaḥpādādinābhyaṃtaṃ klīṃ

pṛ0 164a) kṣaṃklīṃkṣaṃklīṃkṣaṃnamaḥnābhyādimūrdhāṃtaṃ ititṛtīyonyāsaḥ hrīṃ
aṃhrīṃaṃhrīṃaṃnamaḥśirasi hrīṃāṃhrīṃāṃhrīṃāṃnamaḥmukhe hrīṃiṃhrīṃ
iṃhrīṃiṃnamaḥdakṣanetre hrīṃīṃhrīṃīṃhrīṃīṃnamaḥvāme hrīṃuṃhrīṃuṃhrīṃuṃnamaḥ
dakṣakarṇe hrīṃūṃhrīṃūṃhrīṃūṃnamaḥvāme hrīṃṛṃhrīṃṛṃhrīṃṛṃnamaḥdakṣa

gaṃḍe hrīṃḹṃhrīṃḹṃhrīṃḹṃnamaḥvāme hrīṃeṃhrīṃeṃhrīṃeṃnamaḥūrdhvoṣṭe hrīṃaiṃ

pṛ0 164ba) auṃhrīṃnamaḥadhodaṃteṣu hrīṃaṃhrīṃaṃhrīṃaṃnamaḥlalāṭe hrīṃaṃḥhrīṃaṃḥ
hrīṃaṃḥnamaḥjihvāyāṃ hrīṃkaṃhrīṃkaṃhrīṃkaṃnamaḥdakṣavāhumūle hrīṃkhaṃhrīṃkhaṃ
hrīṃkhaṃnamaḥkurpare hrīṃgaṃhrīṃgaṃhrīṃgaṃnamaḥmaṇivaṃdhe hrīṃghaṃhrīṃghaṃhrīṃghaṃnamaḥ
aṃgulimūle hrīṃṅaṃhrīṃṅaṃhrīṃṅaṃnamaḥaṃgulyagre hrīṃcaṃhrīṃcaṃhrīṃcaṃnamaḥvāma

hrīṃjhaṃhrīṃjhaṃhrīṃjhaṃnamaḥaṃgulimūle hrīṃñaṃhrīṃñaṃhrīṃñaṃnamaḥaṃgulyagre hrīṃṭaṃ
hrīṃṭaṃhrīṃṭaṃnamaḥdakṣapādamūle hrīṃṭhaṃhrīṃṭhaṃhrīṃṭhaṃnamaḥjānuni hrīṃḍaṃhrīṃḍaṃhrīṃ

pṛ0 165a) ḍaṃnamaḥgulphe hrīṃḍhaṃhrīṃḍhaṃhrīṃḍhaṃnamaḥaṃgulimūle hrīṃṇaṃhrīṃṇaṃhrīṃṇaṃnamaḥ
aṃgulyagre hrīṃtaṃhrīṃtaṃhrīṃtaṃnamaḥvāmapādamūle hrīṃthaṃhrīṃthaṃhrīṃthaṃnamaḥjā
nuni hrīṃdaṃhrīṃdaṃhrīṃdaṃnamaḥgulphe hrīṃdhaṃhrīṃdhaṃhrīṃdhaṃnamaḥaṃgulimūle hrīṃ
naṃhrīṃnaṃhrīṃnaṃnamaḥaṃgulyagre hrīṃpaṃhrīṃpaṃhrīṃpaṃnamaḥdakṣapārśve hrīṃphaṃhrīṃphaṃ
hrīṃphaṃnamaḥvāmapārśve hrīṃvaṃhrīṃvaṃhrīṃvaṃnamaḥpṛṣṭe hrīṃbhaṃhrīṃbhaṃhrīṃbhaṃnamaḥ

pṛ0 165ba) vāmāṃse hrīṃśaṃhrīṃśaṃhrīṃśaṃnamaḥhṛdādidakṣapāṇyaṃtaṃ hrīṃṣaṃhrīṃṣaṃhrīṃ
ṣaṃnamaḥhṛdādivāmapāṇyaṃtaṃ hrīṃsaṃhrīṃsaṃhrīṃsaṃnamaḥhṛdādidakṣa
pādāṃtaṃ hrīṃhaṃhrīṃhaṃhrīṃhaṃnamaḥhṛdādivāmapādāṃte hrīṃlaṃhrīṃlaṃhrīṃlaṃ
namaḥpādādinābhyaṃtaṃ hrīṃkṣaṃhrīṃkṣaṃhrīṃkṣaṃnamaḥnābhyādimūrdhāṃtaṃ iti
caturthonyāsaḥ hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥśirasi

pṛ0 166a) ḹṃnamaḥvāme hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃdakṣakarṇe hrāṃ
hrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥvāme hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃ
hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥdakṣanāsāpuṭe hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃ
ṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥvāme hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥdakṣagaṃ
ḍe hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥvāme hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃ
mūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥūrdhvoṣṭe hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃ

pṛ0 166ba) daṃteṣu hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥadhodaṃteṣu

ḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥjihvāyāṃ hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃ
hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥkurpare hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃ

pṛ0 167a) ṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥkurpare hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃ
hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥmaṇivaṃdhe hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃ
ḷṃḹṃnamaḥaṃgulimūle hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥ
aṃgulyagre hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥdakṣapādamūle

ḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥgulphe hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃ
ṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥaṃgulimūle hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃ

pṛ0 167ba) namaḥaṃgulyagre hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥvāmapāda-
mūle hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥjānuni hrāṃhrāṃṛṃ
ṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥgulphe hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃ
hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥaṃgulimūle hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃ

rśve hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥpṛṣṭe hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃ

pṛ0 168a) ḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥnābhau hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃ

ṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥhṛdādivāmapāṇyaṃtaṃ hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃ

pṛ0 168ba) ṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥhṛdādidakṣapādāṃtaṃ hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃ
ṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥhṛdādivāmapādāṃtaṃ hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃ
ṛṃḷṃḹṃnamaḥpādādinābhyaṃtaṃ hrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃḷṃḹṃmūlaṃhrāṃhrāṃṛṃṛṃ
ḷṃḹṃnamaḥnābhyādimūrdhāṃtaṃ itipaṃcamonyāsaḥ 5 mūlaṃaṃmūlaṃkṣaṃ

pṛ0 169a) mūlaṃvaṃnamaḥdakṣanāsāpuṭe mūlaṃṛṃmūlaṃlaṃmūlaṃlaṃnamaḥvāme mūlaṃḷṃmū

shauṃīṃuṃūṃdvilakṣakoṭibhedahaṃsādidvyakṣarātmikākhilamaṃtrādhi
devatāyaisakalaphalapradāyaidvikūṭeśvaryaṃvādevyainamaḥsvādhiṣṭhāne 
mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃṛṃṛṃḷṃtrilakṣakoṭibhedavahnyāditryakṣarātmikā
khilamaṃtrādhidevatāyaisakalaphalapradāyaitrikūṭeśvaryaṃvādevyainamaḥ

pṛ0 180a) maṇipūre mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃḹṃeṃaiṃcaturlakṣakoṭibhedacaṃdrādicatu
rakṣarātmikākhilamaṃtrādhidevatāyaisakalaphalapradāyaicatuṣkūṭe
śvaryaṃvādevyainamaḥanāhate mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃoṃauṃaṃaṃḥpaṃcalakṣako
ṭibhedasūryādipaṃcākṣarātmikākhilamaṃtrādhidevatāyaisakalapha
lapradāyaipaṃcakūṭeśvaryaṃvādevyainamaḥviśuddhau mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃkaṃkhaṃ
gaṃṣaṭlakṣakoṭibhedaskaṃdādiṣaḍakṣarātmikākhilamaṃtrādhidevatā
yaisakalaphalapradāyaiṣaṭkūṭeśvaryaṃvādevyainamaḥājñāyāṃ mūlaṃoṃhsauṃ

pṛ0 180ba) shauṃghaṃṅaṃcaṃsaptalakṣakoṭibhedagaṇapatyādisaptākṣarātmikā
khilamaṃtrādhidevatāyaisakalaphalapradāyaisaptakūṭeśvaryaṃvādevyai
namaḥviṃdau mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃchaṃjaṃjhaṃaṣṭalakṣakoṭibhedavaṭukā
yaṣṭākṣarātmikākhilamaṃtrādhidevatāyaisakalaphalapradāyaia
ṣṭakūṭeśvaryaṃvādevyainamaḥardheṃdau mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃñaṃṭaṃṭhaṃnavalakṣa
koṭibhedavrahmādinavākṣarātmikākhilamaṃtrādhidevatāyaisakala
phalapradāyainavakūṭeśvaryaṃvādevyainamaḥrodhinyāṃ mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃḍaṃḍhaṃ

pṛ0 181a) ṇaṃdaśalakṣakoṭibhedaviṣṇvādidaśākṣarātmikākhilamaṃtrādhi
devatāyaisakalaphalapradāyaidaśakūṭeśvaryaṃvādevyainamaḥnāde mūlaṃ
oṃhsauṃshauṃtaṃthaṃdaṃekādaśalakṣakoṭibhedarudrādiekādaśākṣarā
tmikākhilamaṃtrādhidevatāyaisakalaphalapradāyaiekādaśakūṭe-
śvaryaṃvādevyainamaḥnādāṃte mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃdhaṃnaṃpaṃdvādaśalakṣako
ṭibhedavrāhmyādidvādaśākṣarātmikākhilamaṃtrādhidevatāyaisakala
phalapradāyaidvādaśakūṭeśvaryaṃvādevyainamaḥ śaktau mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃphaṃvaṃ

pṛ0 181ba) bhaṃtrayodaśalakṣakoṭibhedalakṣmyāditrayodaśākṣarātmikākhila
maṃtrādhidevatāyaisakalaphalapradāyaitrayodaśakūṭeśvaryaṃvādevyainamaḥ
vyāpikāyāṃ mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃmaṃyaṃraṃcaturdaśalakṣakoṭibhedagauryādi
caturdaśākṣarātmikākhilamaṃtrādhidevatāyaisakalaphalapradāyaica
turdaśakūṭeśvaṃryavādevyainamaḥ samānāsthāne mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃlaṃvaṃśaṃ
paṃcadaśalakṣakoṭibhedadurgādipaṃcadaśākṣarātmikākhilamaṃtrādhide
vatāyaisakalaphalapradāyaipaṃcadaśakūṭeśvaryaṃvādevyainamaḥ unmāyāṃ 

pṛ0 182a) mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃṣaṃsaṃhaṃlaṃkṣaṃṣoḍaśalakṣakoṭibhedatripurādiṣoḍa
śākṣarātmikākhilamaṃtrādhidevatāyaisakalaphalapradāyaiṣoḍa
śakūṭeśvaryaṃvādevyainamaḥdhruvamaṃḍale mūlaṃoṃaiṃhrīṃśrīṃhsauṃshauṃaṃāṃ
ityādikṣāṃtaḥsakalamaṃtrādhidevatāyaiśrīparāṃvādevyainamaḥshauṃhsauṃ
śrīṃhrīṃaiṃoṃ sarvāṃgeṣu itimaṃtranyāsaḥ athadaivatanyāsaḥ mūlaṃoṃ
hsauṃshauṃaṃāṃsahasrakoṭiyoginīkulasevitāyaiṛṣinivṛtyaṃvā
devyainamaḥ dakṣapāde mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃiṃīṃsahasrakoṭiyoginīkula-

pṛ0 182ba) sevitāyaipratiṣṭhāṃvādevyainamaḥvāmapāde mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃṛṃṛṃsa
hasrakoṭitapasvikulasevitāyaividyāṃvādevyainamaḥ dakṣagulphe mū
laṃoṃhsauṃshauṃsahasrakoṭiśāṃtakulasevitāyaiśāṃtyaṃvādevyainamaḥ
vāmagulphe mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃsahasrakoṭimunikulasevitāyaiśāṃtya-
tītaṃvādevyainamaḥdakṣajaṃghāyāṃ mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃeṃaiṃsahasrakoṭidaivata
kulasevitāyaihṛllekhāṃvādevyainamaḥvāmajaṃghāyāṃ mūloṃhsauṃshauṃoṃoṃ
auṃsahasrakoṭirākṣasakulasevitāyaigaganāṃvādevyainamaḥ dakṣajānuni 

pṛ0 183a) mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃaṃaṃḥsahasrakoṭividyādharakulasevitāyairaktāṃvā
devyainamaḥvāmajānuni mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃkaṃkhaṃsahasrakoṭisiddhakula
sevitāyaimahocchuṣmāṃvādevyainamaḥdakṣoru mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃgaṃghaṃsahasra
koṭisādhyakulasevitāyaikarālikāṃvādevyainamaḥvāmoru mūlaṃoṃhsauṃ
shauṃṅaṃcaṃsahasrakoṭiapsarasākulasevitāyaijayāṃvādevyainamaḥdakṣa-
kaṭe mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃchaṃjaṃsahasrakoṭigaṃdharvakulasevitāyaivijayāṃvā
devyainamaḥvāmakaṭe mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃjhaṃñaṃsahasrakoṭiguhyakakulasevi

pṛ0 183ba) tāyaiajitāṃvādevyainamaḥdakṣapārśve mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃṭaṃṭhaṃsahasrakoṭi
yakṣakakulasevitāyaiparājitāṃvādevyainamaḥvāmapārśve mūlaṃoṃhsauṃ
shauṃḍaṃḍhaṃsahasrakoṭikinnarakulasevitāyaivāmāṃvādevyainamaḥ dakṣasta
ne mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃṇaṃtaṃsahasrakoṭipannagakulasevitāyaijyeṣṭāṃvādevyai
namaḥvāmastane mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃthaṃdaṃsahasrakoṭipitṛkulasevitāyairau
dryaṃvādevyainamaḥdakṣavāhumūle mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃdhaṃnaṃsahasrakoṭigaṇeśvara
kulasevitāyaimāyāṃvādevyainamaḥvāmavāhumūle mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃpaṃphaṃsa

pṛ0 184a) hasrakoṭibhairavakulasevitāyaikuṃḍalinyaṃvādevyainamaḥdakṣabhuje 
mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃbaṃbhaṃsahasrakoṭivaṭukakulasevitāyaikālyaṃvāde
vyainamaḥ vāmabhuje mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃmaṃyaṃsahasrakoṭikṣatreśakulasevi
tāyaikālarātryaṃvādevyainamaḥdakṣāṃse mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃraṃlaṃsahasrakoṭi
pramathakulasevitāyaibhagavatyaṃvādevyainamaḥvāmāṃse mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃ
vaṃśaṃsahasrakoṭivrahmakulasevitāyaisarveśvaryaṃvādevyainamaḥdakṣakarṇe 
mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃṣaṃsaṃsahasrakoṭikulasevitāyaisarvājñāṃvādevyainamaḥ

pṛ0 184ba) vāmakarṇe mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃhaṃlaṃsahasrakoṭirudrakulasevitāyaisa
rvakatraṃvādevyainamaḥ bhāle mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃkṣaṃsahasrakoṭicarācarakula
sevitāyaikulaśaktyaṃvādevyainamaḥ vrahmaraṃdhre mūlaṃoṃaiṃhrīṃśrīṃhsauṃshauṃ
aṃāṃityādikṣaṃtaḥsamastadevatādhirūpāyaiśrīparāṃvādevyainamaḥshauṃhsauṃ
śrīṃhrīṃaiṃoṃsarvāṃgeṣu itidaivatanyāsaḥ athamātṛkānyāsaḥ mūloṃ
hsauṃshauṃkaṃ4ṃanaṃtakoṭibhūcarīkulasahitāyaiāṃkṣāṃmaṃgalāṃvādevyaiāṃ
kṣāṃvrahmyaṃvādevyaianaṃtakoṭibhūtasahitāyaaṃkṣaṃmaṃgalanāthāyaaṃkṣaṃa-

pṛ0 185a) sitāṃgabhairavanāthāyanamaḥmūlādhāre mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃcaṃ4ṃanaṃtakoṭi
khecarīkulasahitāyīṃlāṃcarcikāṃvādevyaiīṃlāṃmāheśvaryaṃvādevyaianaṃ
takoṭivetālakulasahitāyiṃlaṃcarcikanāthāyiṃlaṃrurubhairavanāthāya
namaḥsvādhiṣṭāne mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃṭaṃ4ṃanaṃtakoṭipālālacarīkulasahi
tāyūṃhāṃyogīśvaryaṃvādevyaiūṃhāṃkaumārīśvaryaṃvādevyaianaṃtakoṭipiśā
cakulasahitāyuṃhaṃyogīśvarakulanāthāyuṃhaṃcaṃḍabhairavanāthāyanamaḥma
ṇipūre mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃtaṃ4ṃanaṃtakoṭidikcarīkulasahitāyṛṃsāṃhara-

pṛ0 185ba) siddhyaṃvādevyaiṛṃsāṃvaiṣṇavyaṃvādevyaianaṃtakoṭiapasmārakulasahitāya
ṛṃsaṃharasiddhanāthāyṛṃsaṃkrodhabhairavanāthāyanamaḥ anāhate mūlaṃoṃ
hsauṃshauṃpaṃ4ṃanaṃtakoṭisahacarīkulasahitāyaḹṃṣāṃbhaṭṭinyaṃvādevyaiḷṃ
ṣāṃvārāhyaṃvādevyaianaṃtakoṭivrahmarākṣasakulasahitāyaḷṃṣaṃbhaṭṭināthā
yaḷṃṣaṃunmattabhairavanāthāyanamaḥviśuddhe mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃyaṃiṃanaṃtakoṭi
giricarīkulasahitāyaiṃśāṃkilikilyaṃvādevyaiaiṃśāṃiṃdrāṇyaṃvādevyaianaṃ
takoṭiceṭakakulasahitāyeṃśaṃkilakilanāthāyeṃśaṃkapālabhairavanāthā

pṛ0 186a) yanamaḥ ājñāyāṃ mūlaṃoṃhsauṃshauṃśaṃṣaṃsaṃhaṃanaṃtakoṭivanacarīkulase
vitāyauṃvāṃkālarātryaṃvādevyaiauṃvāṃcāmuṃḍādevyaianaṃtakoṭipretakulasa
hitāyoṃvaṃkālarātrināthāyoṃvaṃbhīṣaṇabhairavanāthāyanamaḥbhāle mūlaṃ
oṃhsauṃshauṃlaṃkṣaṃanaṃtakoṭijalacarīkulasahitāyaaṃḥlāṃbhīṣaṇāṃvādevyai
aṃḥlāṃmahālakṣmyaṃvādevyaianaṃtakoṭikūṣmāṃḍakūlasahitāyaaṃlaṃbhīṣaṇa
nāthāyaaṃlaṃsaṃhārabhairavanāthāyanamaḥvrahmaraṃdhre mūlaṃoṃaiṃhrīṃśrīṃhsauṃshauṃaṃ
āṃityādikṣāṃtaḥsamastamātṛbhairavādidevatāyaiśrīparāṃvādevyainamaḥshauṃ

pṛ0 186ba) hsauṃśrīṃhrīṃaiṃoṃsarvāṃgeṣu evaṃnyastatanurdevidhyāyeddevamananyadhīḥ saha
stradalapaṃkajesakalaśītaraśmiprabhaṃvarābhayakarāṃvujaṃvimalagaṃdhapuṣpāṃvaraṃ
prasannavadanekṣaṇaṃsakaladevatārūpiṇaṃsmarecchirasihaṃsagaṃtadapidhyāna-
pūrvaṃguruṃ evaṃnyāsakṛtedevisākṣātparaśivobhavet maṃtrīnacātrasaṃdeho
nigrahānugrahakṣamaḥ mahāṣoḍhāhvayaṃnyāsaṃyaḥkarotidinedine devāsarvena
masyaṃtitaṃnamāminasaṃśayaḥ mahāṣoḍhāhvayaṃnyāsaṃyaḥkarotihipārvati 
divyakṣetraṃsamuddiṣṭaṃsamaṃtāddaśayojanaṃ kṛtvānyāsamimaṃdeviyatragacchatisādhakaḥ 

pṛ0 187a) tatraśrīrvijayolābhaḥsātmānaṃpauruṣaḥpriye mahāṣoḍhākṛtanyāsatadajñaṃ
yadivaṃdate māsānmṛtyumavāpnotiyaditrātāśivaḥsvayaṃ vajrapaṃjaranāmā
namevaṃnyāsaṃkarotiyaḥ divyaṃtarikṣabhūśailajalāraṇyanivāsinaḥ uddaṃḍa
bhūtavetāladevarakṣograhādayaḥ bhayagrastenamanasānekṣaṃtetaṃmaheśvari ma
hāṣoḍhāhvayaṃnyāsaṃvrahmaviṣṇuśivādayaḥ devāḥsarvepikurvaṃtiṛṣiyogi
munīśvaraḥ vahunoktenakiṃdevinyāsametaṃmamapriye nāputrāyavadeddevinā
śiṣyāyaprakāśayet ajñāyāṃlabhatesiddhiṃrahasinyāsamācaret asmā

pṛ0 187ba) tparatamaḥsākṣāddevatābhāvasiddhidaḥ lokenāstinasaṃdehaḥsatyaṃsatyaṃ
vadāmyahaṃ urdhvāmnāyapraveśaścaparāprāsādaciṃtanaṃ mahāṣoḍhāparijñā
naṃnālpasyatapasaḥphalam itimahāṣoḍhānyāsaḥ athavidhivihitapa-
dmādyāsanomūlādhārārpitacittavṛttirmūlamaṃtraṃ praṇavaṃvāekaguṇaṃsmara
nniḍayāvāyumāpūryamūlādhāraṃprāpayyāṃguṣṭānāmākaniṣṭābhirnāsāpuṭo
tsṛṣṭataṃvāyuṃkubhaṃyitvā kubhaṃkasamayemūlaṃpraṇavaṃvācaturguṇaṃjapanpunaḥ piṃ
galayādviguṇaṃtamevajapanvāyuṃśanairecayet ityekaḥprāṇāyāmaḥ punaryayā

pṛ0 188a) recitaṃtayaivapunarāpūryatathaivakubhaṃyitvānyatorecayedityanyaḥ evameva
vaiparītyenapunaḥpunarviṃśativāraṃ ṣoḍaśa dvādaśaṣaṭvāraṃvājapādauja
pāṃtecaprāṇāyāmaḥkāryaḥ itiprāṇāyāmaṃkṛtvā mūlavidyāyāṛṣyā
dismaraṇaṃkuryāt oṃasyaśrīmahātripurasuṃdarīmaṃtrarājasya śrīdakṣi-
ṇāmūrtiṛṣiḥ paṃktichaṃdaḥ śrīmahātripurasuṃdarīdevatā aiṃprathamakūṭaṃ
vījaṃ sauṃḥtṛtīyakūṭaṃśaktiḥ klīṃdvitīyakūṭaṃkīlakaṃ mamacaturvidhapuru
ṣārthasiddhayenyāsādauviniyogaḥ evaṃṛṣyādinyāsaḥ athakaranyāsaḥ 

pṛ0 188ba) aiṃprathamakūṭaṃaṃguṣṭābhyāṃnamaḥ klīṃdvitīyakūṭaṃtarjanībhyāṃsvāhā 
sauṃḥtṛtīyakūṭaṃmadhyamābhyāṃvaṣaṭ aiṃprathamakūṭaṃanāmikābhyāṃhuṃ klīṃ
dvitīyakūṭaṃkaniṣṭikābhyāṃvauṣaṭ sauṃḥtṛtīyakūṭakaratalakarapṛṣṭābhyāṃ
phaṭ itikaranyāsaḥ evaṃṣaḍaṃganyāsaḥ śrīṣoḍaśārṇopāsakaiḥ śrīṃhrīṃ
klīṃaiṃsauṃḥhṛdayāyanamaḥ oṃhrīṃśrīṃśirasesvāhā keīlahrīṃśikhā-
yaivaṣaṭ hasakahalahrīṃkavacāyahuṃ sakalahrīṃnetratrayāyavauṣaṭ sauṃḥ
aiṃklīṃśrīṃastrāyaphaṭ itiṣaḍaṃgaḥ astreṇatālatrayaṃdatvā choṭikā-

pṛ0 189a) bhiḥdaśadigvaṃdhanaṃkuryāt athayogapīṭhanyāsaḥ tatrāṃsadvayorudva
yakalpitapādacatuṣṭayaṃyogapīṭhaṃnijadehaṃdhyātvānyaset oṃatha
dhyānam śarīraṃpaṃcasaṃbhūtaṃsarveṃdriyasamāvṛtaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśatattvasaṃpūrṇaṃsa
rvabhogālayaṃśubhaṃ ṣaṭcakraṃṣoḍaśādhāraṃtrilakṣyaṃvyomapaṃcakaṃ tatpīṭhaṃsa
rvapīṭhānāmālayaṃsarvadāciteḥ evaṃnijavapurjñeyaṃpūjanīyaṃkulānvayaṃ 
tatastasminnyasedvīrovigatāśeṣakalmaṣaḥ oṃaiṃhrīṃśrīṃmaṃḍūkāyanamaḥ
mūlādhāre 4ṃkālāgnirudrāyanamaḥsvādhiṣṭāne 4ṃmūlaprakṛtyainamaḥ

pṛ0 189ba) maṇipūre 4ṃādhāraśaktyainamaḥ anāhate 4ṃkūrmāyanamaḥ hṛdaye 
tatraiva 4anaṃtāyanamaḥ 4ṃvarāhāyanamaḥ 4pṛthivyainamaḥ 4ṃpaścimā
divilomena 4ṃikṣurasasamudrāyanamaḥ 4ṃmadirāsamudrāyanamaḥ 4ṃghṛ
tasamudrāyanamaḥ 4ṃdugdhasamudrāyanamaḥ tanmadhye 4ṃkṣīrasamudrāyanamaḥ 
4ṃdadhisamudrāyanamaḥ 4ṃlavaṇasamudrāyanamaḥ 4ṃaṃāṃityādikṣāṃtāna
vakhaṃḍavirājitāyanavaratnamayadvīpāyanamaḥ tatpaścime aṃāṃiṃīṃuṃ
ūṃṛṃṛṃpuṣparatnāyanamaḥ naiṛte ḷṃḹṃeṃaiṃoṃauṃaṃaṃḥvaiḍūryaratnāyanamaḥ 

pṛ0 190a) dakṣiṇe kaṃ4ṃnīlaratnāyanamaḥ āgneye caṃ4ṃvidrumaratnāyanamaḥ pū
rve ṭaṃ4ṃmauktikaratnāyanamaḥ īśāne taṃ4ṃmarakataratnāyanamaḥ uttare 
paṃ4ṃvajraratnāyanamaḥvāyavye yaṃiṃgomedharatnāyanamaḥ madhyakhaṃḍe 
śaṃṣaṃsaṃhaṃpuṣparāgaratnāyanamaḥ laṃkṣaṃḥpadmarāgaratnāyanamaḥ tanmadhya
khaṃḍamadhye oṃaiṃhrīṃśrīṃsvarṇaparvatāyanamaḥ tadupari 4ṃnaṃdanodyānāya
namaḥ tanmadhye 4ṃkalpavṛkṣebhyonamaḥ tanmadhye 4ṃvasaṃtādiṣaṭṛtu-
bhyonamaḥ tatpaścime 4ṃiṃdriyāśvebhyonamaḥ tatpūrve 4ṃiṃdriyārthagajebhyo

pṛ0 190ba) namaḥ tanmadhye 4ṃvicitraratnabhūmikāyainamaḥ tasyāḥpaścime4ṃkālaca
kreśvaryainamaḥ nairṛte 4ṃmudrācakreśvaryainamaḥ dakṣiṇe 4ṃmātṛcakreśvaryaina
maḥ āgneye 4ṃratnacakreśvaryainamaḥ pūrve 4ṃdeśacakreśvaryainamaḥ īśāne guru
cakreśvaryainamaḥ uttare 4ṃtattvacakreśvaryainamaḥvāyavye 4ṃgrahacakreśvaryainamaḥ 
madhye 4ṃkaruṇātoyaparidhayenamaḥ svarṇaprākārāyanamaḥ tanmadhye maṇimaṃḍa
pāyanamaḥ tasyanairṛtyādicatuṣkoṇeṣuvāmāvartena 4ṃ kālarūpiṇīśaktyai
namaḥ 4ṃdeśarūpiṇīśaktyainamaḥ 4ṃ ākāśarūpiṇīśaktyainamaḥ 4ṃśavdarū-

pṛ0 191a) piṇīśaktyainamaḥ madhye saṃgītayoginībhyonamaḥ tatraiva 4ṃsamastagu

namaḥ vāmorau vairāgyāyanamaḥdakṣorau aiśvaryāyanamaḥ mukhe adharmāyana-

pṛ0 191ba) maḥ vāmapārśve ajñānāyanamaḥ nābhau avairāgyāyanamaḥ dakṣapārśve

pṛ0 192a) ātmanenamaḥ aṃaṃtarātmanenamaḥ paṃparamātmanenamaḥ hrīṃjñānātmane

pṛ0 192ba) vinyasya prathamakūṭaṃśarīrātmanenamaḥ dvitīyakūṭaṃvuddhyātmanenamaḥ

pṛ0 193a) dakṣalalāṭabhāge hrīṃklīṃsauṃḥtripurasuṃdarīdevyātmāsanāyanamaḥ dakṣa

ṇeṣu hlīṃklīṃvlīṃtripuramālinīsādhyasiddhāsanāyanamaḥ 4ṃaṃkaṃkhaṃgaṃ
ghaṃṅaṃcaṃchaṃjaṃjhaṃñaṃṭaṃṭhaṃḍaṃḍhaṃṇaṃtaṃthaṃdaṃdhanaṃpaṃphaṃvaṃbhaṃmaṃyaṃraṃlaṃvaṃśaṃṣaṃsaṃhaṃlaṃkṣaṃḥ

pṛ0 193ba) pādapāyūpasthaśavdasparśarūparasagaṃdhākāśavāyvagnisalilapṛthivyā

pṛ0 194a) npūjyāsadādevīsarvarājopacārakaiḥ tatonyāsaṃprakurvītaśrīcakrākā

pṛ0 195a) namaḥ pṛṣṭavaṃśe 4ṃsarvakāmasiddhyainamaḥ cūḍādhaḥ itivinyasya 4ṃcatura

pṛ0 195ba) maḥ dakṣapārśve 4ṃklīṃsarvākarṣiṇyainamaḥ mūrdhni 4ṃvlūṃsarvavaśyāyaina

pṛ0 196a) lokyamohanecakresamudrāḥsasiddhayaḥsāyudhāḥsavāhanāḥsapari

pṛ0 197a) vāmāṃse 4ṃaṃamṛtākarṣiṇīnityākalāyainamaḥ vāmakarṇapṛṣṭe

pṛ0 199a) yoginyaḥsarvasaubhāgyadāyakecakresamudrāḥsasiddhayāḥsāyudhāḥ

pṛ0 201a) 4ṃaṃāityādiaṃḥvlūṃvaśinīvāgdevatāyainamaḥ kaṃṭhadakṣabhāge

pṛ0 202a) saḥyāṃrāṃlāṃvāṃjṛṃbhanebhyaḥkāmeśvarakāmeśvarīvāṇebhyonamaḥ 4ṃdhāṃdhāṃ

pṛ0 220a) rthaṃvījaṃnamaḥ ligeṃ 4ṃpaṃcamaṃvījaṃnamaḥ pṛṣṭe 4ṃṣaṣṭaṃvījaṃnamaḥ nā

pṛ0 220ba) netrayoḥ 4ṃtṛtīyaṃvījaṃnamaḥ karṇayoḥ 4ṃcaturthaṃvījaṃnamaḥ ghrāṇa

mastavidyāyāvyāpakaṃnyaset itisṛṣṭinyāsaḥ athasthitinyāsaḥ 

pṛ0 221a) karāṃguṣṭayoḥ 4ṃprathamaṃvījaṃnamaḥ tarjanyoḥ 4ṃdvitīyaṃvījaṃnamaḥ

pṛ0 221ba) 4ṃpaṃcadaśaṃvījaṃnamaḥ tatkaniṣṭayoḥ 4ṃṣoḍaśaṃvījaṃnamaḥ itiṣoḍa
śīvarṇaiḥsthitinyāsaḥ athaṣoḍaśārṇāyāpaṃcavidhavarṇanyāsaḥ

pṛ0 222a) namaḥ mukhe trayodaśaṃvījaṃnamaḥ ūrdhvadaṃteṣu 4ṃcaturdaśaṃvījaṃnamaḥ

kaḥprakāraḥ 1 śirasi 4ṃprathamaṃvījaṃnamaḥ sīmaṃte 4ṃdvitīyaṃvījaṃ
namaḥ lalāṭe 4ṃtṛtīyaṃvījaṃnamaḥ bhrūdvaye 4ṃcaturthaṃvījaṃnamaḥ nāsā

pṛ0 222ba) jaṃnamaḥ vāmavāhumūle 4ṃdvādaśaṃvījaṃnamaḥ tanmadhye 4ṃtrayoda

pṛ0 223a) maṃvījaṃnamaḥ aṃgulyagre 4ṃekādaśaṃvījaṃnamaḥ vāmorumūle 4ṃdvā

ṇe 4ṃsaptamaṃvījaṃnamaḥ vāme 4ṃaṣṭamaṃvījaṃnamaḥ dakṣagaṃḍe 4ṃnavamaṃvī-

pṛ0 223ba) jaṃnamaḥ vāme 4ṃdaśamaṃvījaṃnamaḥ oṣṭe 4ṃekādaśaṃvījaṃnamaḥ a

pṛ0 224a) 4ṃnavamaṃvījaṃnamaḥ hṛdi 4ṃdaśamaṃvījaṃnamaḥ vrahmaraṃdhre 4ṃekādaśaṃ

pṛ0 224ba) 4ṃāṃhrīṃvaśīkaraṇābhyāṃkāmeśvarakāmeśvarīpāśābhyāṃnamaḥ dakṣo

ayaṃtanmudrayākāryaḥ ityāyudhanyāyaḥ athakāmeśvaryādinyāsaḥ

nābhyādikaṃṭhaparyaṃtaṃ klīṃdvitīyakūṭaṃvajreśvaryainamaḥ kaṃṭhāchiraḥ
paryaṃtaṃ sauṃḥtṛtīyakūṭaṃbhagamālinyainamaḥ itivāmabhāgevinyasya 
dakṣapādāṃguṣṭānnābhiparyaṃtaṃhsaiṃātmatatvādhipatayevrahmaṇenamaḥ 

pṛ0 225a) nābhyādikaṃṭhaparyaṃtaṃ hsklrīṃvidyātattvādhipatayeviṣṇavenamaḥ 
kaṃṭhādiśiroṃtaṃ hsrauṃśivatatvādhipatayenamaḥ itidakṣabhāgevi
nyasya itikāmeśvaryādinyāsaḥ athacattvastattvanyāsaḥ nābhyā-
dipādāṃtaṃ 4ṃprathamakūṭaṃātmatatvātmaneśrīmahātripurasuṃdaryai
namaḥ kaṃṭhādinābhyaṃtaṃ 4ṃdvitīyakūṭaṃvidyātatvātmaneśrīmahātripu
rasuṃdaryainamaḥ mūrdhādikaṃṭhāṃtaṃ 4ṃtṛtīyakūṭaṃśivatatvātmaneśrī-
mahātripurasuṃdaryainamaḥ sarvāṃge 4ṃturīyavidyāsarvatatvātmaneśrī-

pṛ0 225ba) mahātripurasuṃdaryainamaḥ iticatustattvanyāsaḥ athamūlavidyānyā

nyāsaḥ vāmakarṇe 4ṃaiṃnamaḥ dakṣakarṇe 4ṃklīṃnamaḥ civuke 4ṃsauṃḥna

pṛ0 226a) namaḥ dakṣāṃse 4ṃklīṃnamaḥ hṛdaye 4ṃsauṃḥnamaḥ vāmakurpare 4ṃaiṃ
